21: function SaaS_DnevnikMasine({ modul, header, user, saas, updatePolje, toggleVelicina, spremiDimenzije, handleDragStart, handleDragEnter, handleDrop, prikaziDialog, zatvoriDialog }) {
22:     const [logovi, setLogovi] = useState([]);
23:     const t = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('de-DE', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
24:     const [form, setForm] = useState({ vrijeme_od: t, vrijeme_do: '', zastoj_min: '', napomena: '' });
25: 
26:     useEffect(() => { loadLogove(); }, [header]);
27:     const loadLogove = async () => {
28:         if(!header || !header.datum || !header.masina) return;
29:         const { data } = await supabase.from('dnevnik_masine').select('*').eq('datum', header.datum).eq('masina', header.masina).eq('modul', modul).order('vrijeme_od', { ascending: false });
30:         if (data) setLogovi(data);
31:     };
32:     const snimiZastojIliRad = async () => {
33:         if (!form.vrijeme_od) return toast.warning("Vrijeme početka je obavezno!");
34:         const payload = {
35:             datum: header.datum, masina: header.masina, modul: modul,
36:             vrijeme_od: form.vrijeme_od, vrijeme_do: form.vrijeme_do || null,
37:             zastoj_min: parseInt(form.zastoj_min) || 0, napomena: form.napomena, snimio: user?.ime_prezime || 'Nepoznat'
38:         };
39:         const { error } = await supabase.from('dnevnik_masine').insert([payload]);
40:         if (error) return toast.error("Greška: " + error.message);
41:         toast.success("Zapis o radu/zastoju uspješno dodan!");
42:         setForm({ vrijeme_od: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('de-DE', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }), vrijeme_do: '', zastoj_min: '', napomena: '' });
43:         loadLogove();
44:     };
45: 
46:     const obrisiLog = (id) => {
47:         prikaziDialog({
48:             tip: 'upozorenje',
49:             naslov: 'Brisanje zapisa zastoja',
50:             poruka: 'Da li ste sigurni da želite obrisati ovaj zapis iz dnevnika mašine?',
51:             confirmText: '🗑️ OBRIŠI',
52:             cancelText: '✕ ODUSTANI',
53:             onConfirm: async () => {
54:                 await supabase.from('dnevnik_masine').delete().eq('id', id);
55:                 loadLogove();
56:                 zatvoriDialog();
57:                 toast.info("Zapis obrisan iz dnevnika.");
58:             },
59:             onCancel: zatvoriDialog
60:         });
61:     };
62: 
63:     const renderDnevnikPolje = (polje) => {
64:         if (polje.id === 'pocetak') return <input type="time" value={form.vrijeme_od} onChange={e => setForm({...form, vrijeme_od: e.target.value})} className="w-full h-full min-h-[45px] p-3 bg-theme-panel rounded-xl text-xs text-theme-text border border-theme-border outline-none focus:border-amber-500" />;
65:         if (polje.id === 'kraj') return <input type="time" value={form.vrijeme_do} onChange={e => setForm({...form, vrijeme_do: e.target.value})} className="w-full h-full min-h-[45px] p-3 bg-theme-panel rounded-xl text-xs text-theme-text border border-theme-border outline-none focus:border-amber-500" />;
66:         if (polje.id === 'zastoj') return <input type="number" value={form.zastoj_min} onChange={e => setForm({...form, zastoj_min: e.target.value})} placeholder="0" className="w-full h-full min-h-[45px] p-3 bg-red-900/20 rounded-xl text-xs text-red-400 font-black border border-red-500/50 outline-none text-center" />;
67:         if (polje.id === 'napomena') return <input type="text" value={form.napomena} onChange={e => setForm({...form, napomena: e.target.value})} placeholder="Održavanje, kvar..." className="w-full h-full min-h-[45px] p-3 bg-theme-panel rounded-xl text-xs text-theme-text border border-theme-border outline-none focus:border-amber-500" />;
68:         return null;
69:     };
70: 
71:     return (
72:         <div className={`bg-theme-card backdrop-blur-[var(--glass-blur)] p-6 rounded-[2.5rem] border shadow-2xl space-y-4 mt-6 transition-all ${saas.isEditMode ? 'ring-2 ring-amber-500 border-amber-500/50' : 'border-theme-border'}`} style={{ backgroundColor: saas.ui.boja_kartice || undefined }}>
73:             <h3 className="text-amber-500 font-black uppercase text-xs">⚙️ EVIDENCIJA RADA I ZASTOJA MAŠINE</h3>
74:             <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 bg-theme-panel p-4 rounded-2xl border border-theme-accent/40 items-start shadow-inner">
75:                 {(saas.ui.polja_dnevnik || []).map((polje, index) => {
76:                     const responsiveSpan = polje.span === 'col-span-4' ? 'col-span-1 sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-4' : (polje.span === 'col-span-2' ? 'col-span-1 sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-2' : 'col-span-1');
77:                     return (
78:                         <div 
79:                             key={polje.id} 
80:                             className={`relative flex flex-col ${responsiveSpan} w-full transition-all ${saas.isEditMode ? 'border-2 border-dashed border-amber-500 p-2 rounded-xl bg-black/20 resize overflow-auto' : ''}`} 
81:                             style={{ maxWidth: '100%', ...(saas.isEditMode ? { minWidth: '100px', minHeight: '80px', width: polje.customWidth || undefined, height: polje.customHeight || undefined } : {}) }}
82:                             draggable={saas.isEditMode} 
83:                             onDragStart={(e) => handleDragStart(e, index, 'polja_dnevnik')} 
84:                             onDragEnter={(e) => handleDragEnter(e, index)} 
85:                             onDragEnd={() => handleDrop('polja_dnevnik')} 
86:                             onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
87:                             onMouseUp={(e) => spremiDimenzije(e, index, 'polja_dnevnik')}
88:                         >
89:                             {saas.isEditMode && (
90:                                 <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2 shrink-0">
91:                                     <span className="text-[9px] text-amber-500 uppercase font-black cursor-move">☰</span>
92:                                     <button onClick={() => toggleVelicina(index, 'polja_dnevnik')} className="text-[8px] text-amber-500 font-black bg-amber-500/20 px-2 py-1 rounded">ŠIRINA: {polje.span==='col-span-4'?'100%':polje.span==='col-span-2'?'50%':'25%'}</button>
93:                                 </div>
94:                             )}
95:                             {saas.isEditMode ? (
96:                                 <input value={polje.label} onChange={(e) => updatePolje(index, 'label', e.target.value, 'polja_dnevnik')} className="w-full bg-theme-card text-amber-400 p-1 mb-1 rounded border border-amber-500/50 text-[8px] uppercase font-black text-center shrink-0" placeholder="Ostavite prazno za bez naslova" />
97:                             ) : (
98:                                 polje.label && <label className="text-[8px] text-slate-500 uppercase ml-2 block mb-1 shrink-0">{polje.label}</label>
99:                             )}
100:                             <div className={`flex-1 w-full ${saas.isEditMode ? 'opacity-50 pointer-events-none' : ''}`}>{renderDnevnikPolje(polje)}</div>
101:                         </div>
102:                     );
103:                 })}
104:                 <button onClick={snimiZastojIliRad} className={`w-full py-3.5 bg-amber-600 text-white font-black rounded-xl text-[10px] uppercase shadow-lg hover:bg-amber-500 ${saas.isEditMode ? 'opacity-50 pointer-events-none col-span-1 sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-4' : 'col-span-1 sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-4 mt-2'}`}>➕ Dodaj Zapis u Dnevnik</button>
105:             </div>
106:             
107:             <div className="space-y-2 mt-4 max-h-40 overflow-y-auto pr-2 custom-scrollbar">
108:                 {logovi.length === 0 && <p className="text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] uppercase">Nema unesenih zastoja za danas.</p>}
109:                 {logovi.map(l => (
110:                     <div key={l.id} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between sm:items-center p-3 bg-theme-panel border border-theme-border rounded-xl gap-2">
111:                         <div>
112:                             <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-black"><span className="text-emerald-400">{l.vrijeme_od}</span> - {l.vrijeme_do ? <span className="text-amber-400">{l.vrijeme_do}</span> : <span className="text-slate-500">...</span>}</p>
113:                             <p className="text-theme-text text-xs font-bold mt-1">{l.napomena || 'Nema napomene'}</p>
114:                         </div>

116:                             {l.zastoj_min > 0 && <span className="text-red-400 text-xs font-black bg-red-900/30 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-red-500/30">Zastoj: {l.zastoj_min} min</span>}
117:                             <button onClick={() => obrisiLog(l.id)} className="text-red-500 font-black hover:bg-slate-700 p-2 rounded shrink-0 border border-red-500/30 sm:border-none">✕ Obriši</button>
118:                         </div>
119:                     </div>
120:                 ))}
121:             </div>
122:         </div>
123:     );

126: export default function DoradaModule({ user, header, setHeader, onExit }) {
127:     
128:     useEffect(() => {
129:         const postaviMasinu = async () => {
130:             const { data } = await supabase.from('masine').select('naziv').ilike('dozvoljeni_moduli', '%Dorada%');
131:             const dozvoljene = data ? data.map(m => m.naziv) : [];
132:             const zadnja = localStorage.getItem('zadnja_masina_dorada');
133: 
134:             if (!header?.masina || !dozvoljene.includes(header.masina)) {
135:                 const nova = (zadnja && dozvoljene.includes(zadnja)) ? zadnja : (dozvoljene[0] || 'VIŠELISNI CIRKULAR');
136:                 setHeader(prev => ({ ...prev, masina: nova }));
137:                 localStorage.setItem('zadnja_masina_dorada', nova);
138:             } else if (header.masina && dozvoljene.includes(header.masina)) {
139:                 localStorage.setItem('zadnja_masina_dorada', header.masina);
140:             }
141:         };
142:         postaviMasinu();
143:     }, [header?.masina]);
144: 
145:     const saas = useSaaS('dorada_modul', {
146:         boja_kartice: '#1e293b',
147:         boja_slova: '#ffffff',
148:         velicina_naslova: '16',
149:         boja_teksta_ulaz: 'text-red-500',
150:         boja_teksta_izlaz: 'text-emerald-500',
151:         naslov_ulaz: 'SKENIRAJ ULAZNI PAKET (SIROVINA - Dimenzije u cm)',
152:         naslov_izlaz: 'QR IZLAZNOG PAKETA (GOTOVO - Dimenzije u cm)',
153:         polja_radnici: [
154:             { id: 'operater', label: '👨‍🔧 ODGOVORNI RADNIK (ZA MAŠINOM)', span: 'col-span-1' },
155:             { id: 'viljuskarista', label: '🚜 VILJUŠKARISTA', span: 'col-span-1' }
156:         ],
157:         polja_dnevnik: [
158:             { id: 'pocetak', label: 'POČETAK (SATI)', span: 'col-span-1' },
159:             { id: 'kraj', label: 'ZAVRŠETAK (SATI)', span: 'col-span-1' },
160:             { id: 'zastoj', label: 'ZASTOJ (MINUTA)', span: 'col-span-1' },
161:             { id: 'napomena', label: 'NAPOMENA / RAZLOG', span: 'col-span-4' }
162:         ]
163:     });
164:     const dragItem = useRef(null);
165:     const dragOverItem = useRef(null);
166: 
167:     const handleDragStart = (e, index, lista) => { dragItem.current = { index, lista }; };
168:     const handleDragEnter = (e, index) => { dragOverItem.current = index; };
169:     const handleDrop = (listaIme) => {
170:         if(!dragItem.current || dragOverItem.current === null || dragItem.current.lista !== listaIme) return;
171:         const aktuelnaLista = saas.ui[listaIme]?.length > 0 ? saas.ui[listaIme] : saas.defaultConfig[listaIme];
172:         const novaLista = [...aktuelnaLista];
173:         const premjesteniItem = novaLista[dragItem.current.index];
174:         novaLista.splice(dragItem.current.index, 1);
175:         novaLista.splice(dragOverItem.current, 0, premjesteniItem);
176:         dragItem.current = null; dragOverItem.current = null;
177:         saas.setUi({...saas.ui, [listaIme]: novaLista});
178:     };
179:     const updatePolje = (index, key, val, listaIme) => {
180:         const aktuelnaLista = saas.ui[listaIme]?.length > 0 ? saas.ui[listaIme] : saas.defaultConfig[listaIme];
181:         const novaLista = [...aktuelnaLista];
182:         novaLista[index][key] = val;
183:         saas.setUi({...saas.ui, [listaIme]: novaLista});
184:     };
185:     const toggleVelicinaPolja = (index, listaIme) => {
186:         const aktuelnaLista = saas.ui[listaIme]?.length > 0 ? saas.ui[listaIme] : saas.defaultConfig[listaIme];
187:         const novaLista = [...aktuelnaLista];
188:         const trenutno = novaLista[index].span;
189:         novaLista[index].span = trenutno === 'col-span-1' ? 'col-span-2' : (trenutno === 'col-span-2' ? 'col-span-4' : 'col-span-1');
190:         saas.setUi({...saas.ui, [listaIme]: novaLista});
191:     };
192:     const spremiDimenzije = (e, index, listaIme) => {
193:         if (!saas.isEditMode) return;
194:         const w = e.currentTarget.style.width;
195:         const h = e.currentTarget.style.height;
196:         if (w || h) {
197:             const aktuelnaLista = saas.ui[listaIme]?.length > 0 ? saas.ui[listaIme] : saas.defaultConfig[listaIme];
198:             const novaLista = [...aktuelnaLista];
199:             if (w) novaLista[index].customWidth = w;
200:             if (h) novaLista[index].customHeight = h;
201:             saas.setUi({...saas.ui, [listaIme]: novaLista});
202:         }
203:     };

261:     const handleOperaterChange = async (novoIme) => {
262:         if (header?.masina) {
263:             await supabase.from('aktivni_radnici').update({ vrijeme_odjave: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('masina_naziv', header.masina).eq('uloga', 'operater_dorada').is('vrijeme_odjave', null);
264:             if (novoIme) await supabase.from('aktivni_radnici').insert([{ radnik_ime: novoIme, masina_naziv: header.masina, vrijeme_prijave: new Date().toISOString(), uloga: 'operater_dorada' }]);

271:             await supabase.from('aktivni_radnici').update({ vrijeme_odjave: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('masina_naziv', header.masina).eq('uloga', 'viljuskarista_dorada').is('vrijeme_odjave', null);
272:             if (novoIme) await supabase.from('aktivni_radnici').insert([{ radnik_ime: novoIme, masina_naziv: header.masina, vrijeme_prijave: new Date().toISOString(), uloga: 'viljuskarista_dorada' }]);

278:     useEffect(() => {
279:         supabase.from('radnici').select('ime_prezime').then(({data}) => setRadniciList(data ? data.map(r=>({naziv: r.ime_prezime})) : []));
280:         supabase.from('katalog_proizvoda').select('*').then(({data}) => setKatalog(data || []));
281:         supabase.from('radni_nalozi').select('id, kupac_naziv, status, tip_naloga, stavke_jsonb, tehnologija_jsonb').neq('status', 'ZAVRŠENO').then(({data}) => setAktivniNalozi(data || []));
282:         ucitajSveZapoocetePakete();
283:     }, [header?.masina]);

285:     const ucitajSveZapoocetePakete = async () => {
286:         if (!header?.masina) { setActiveIzlazIds([]); setAktivniPaketiInfo([]); return; }
287:         
288:         const { data, error } = await supabase.from('paketi')
289:             .select('paket_id, debljina, sirina, duzina, naziv_proizvoda, kolicina_final, kolicina_m3')
290:             .is('closed_at', null)
291:             .eq('masina', header.masina);
292: 
293:         if (error) {
294:             console.error("Greška pri povlačenju paketa dorade:", error);
295:             return;
296:         }
297: 
298:         if (data && data.length > 0) {
299:             const jedinstveniIds = [...new Set(data.map(p => p.paket_id))];
300:             setActiveIzlazIds(jedinstveniIds);
301:             
302:             const grouped = data.reduce((acc, curr) => {
303:                 if (!acc[curr.paket_id]) {
304:                     acc[curr.paket_id] = { 
305:                         id: curr.paket_id, 
306:                         dimenzija: `${curr.debljina}x${curr.sirina}x${curr.duzina} cm`, 
307:                         proizvod: curr.naziv_proizvoda,
308:                         m3: 0, 
309:                         stavke: 0 
310:                     };
311:                 }
312:                 acc[curr.paket_id].m3 += parseFloat(curr.kolicina_final || curr.kolicina_m3 || 0);
313:                 acc[curr.paket_id].stavke += 1;
314:                 return acc;
315:             }, {});
316:             
317:             setAktivniPaketiInfo(Object.values(grouped).sort((a,b) => b.id.localeCompare(a.id)));
318:         } else {
319:             setActiveIzlazIds([]);
320:             setAktivniPaketiInfo([]);
321:         }
322:     };
323: 
324:     useEffect(() => {
325:         const fetchMasinaAtribute = async () => {
326:             if (!header?.masina) return;
327:             const { data } = await supabase.from('masine').select('atributi_paketa').eq('naziv', header.masina).maybeSingle();
328:             setDostupneOznake(data?.atributi_paketa || []);
329:             setOdabraneOznake([]);

333:         const ucitajDezurneRadnike = async () => {

336:             if (data) {
337:                 const o = data.find(r => r.uloga === 'operater_dorada');
338:                 const v = data.find(r => r.uloga === 'viljuskarista_dorada');
339:                 if (o) { setOperater(o.radnik_ime); localStorage.setItem('dorada_operater', o.radnik_ime); }
340:                 if (v) { setViljuskarista(v.radnik_ime); localStorage.setItem('dorada_viljuskarista', v.radnik_ime); }
341:             }
342:         };
343:         ucitajDezurneRadnike();
344:     }, [header?.masina]);
345: 
346:     useEffect(() => {
347:         const fetchSve = async () => {
348:             if (!header?.masina) return;
349:             
350:             const { data: paks } = await supabase.from('paketi')
351:                 .select('*')
352:                 .gt('kolicina_final', 0)
353:                 .is('closed_at', null)
354:                 .neq('masina', header.masina)
355:                 .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
356:                 .limit(100);
357:

359: 
360:             let validni = paks || [];
361: 
362:             if (radniNalog && tipNaloga === 'FAZNI') {
363:                 validni = validni.filter(p => p.broj_veze === radniNalog);
364:                 validni = validni.filter(p => {
365:                     const katItem = katalog.find(k => k.naziv === p.naziv_proizvoda);
366:                     if (!katItem) return true; 
367:                     
368:                     const bom = techData?.find(t => t.finalna_sifra === katItem.sifra);
369:                     if (!bom || !bom.faze_jsonb) return true; 
370:                     
371:                     const currIdx = bom.faze_jsonb.findIndex(f => f.masina.toUpperCase() === header.masina.toUpperCase());
372:                     if (currIdx > 0) {
373:                         const prevMasina = bom.faze_jsonb[currIdx - 1].masina.toUpperCase();
374:                         return p.masina?.toUpperCase() === prevMasina;
375:                     }
376:                     return true;
377:                 });
378:             }
379: 
380:             setPredlozeniPaketi(validni);
381:         };
382:         fetchSve();
383:     }, [header?.masina, radniNalog, tipNaloga, katalog]);
384: 
385:     const handleNalogSelect = async (val) => {
386:         if(!val) {
387:             setRadniNalog('');
388:             setTipNaloga(''); setRnStavke([]); setPredlozeniPaketi([]); ucitajSveZapoocetePakete();
389:             return;
390:         }
391:         setRadniNalog(val);
392:         const {data} = await supabase.from('radni_nalozi').select('*').eq('id', val.toUpperCase()).maybeSingle();
393:         if (data) {
394:             setTipNaloga(data.tip_naloga || '');
395:             const ucitaneStavke = data.stavke_jsonb || data.stavke || [];
396:             const tehnologija = data.tehnologija_jsonb || {};
397:             const isFazni = data.tip_naloga === 'FAZNI';
398:             let mapiraneStavke = [];
399: 
400:             if (ucitaneStavke.length > 0) {
401:                 ucitaneStavke.forEach(s => {
402:                     const katItem = katalog.find(k => k.sifra === s.sifra) || {};
403:                     let dimenzije = katItem.sifra ? `${katItem.visina || '-'}x${katItem.sirina || '-'}x${katItem.duzina || '-'}` : 'Nema u katalogu';
404:                     let naruceno = parseFloat(s.kolicina_obracun || s.kolicina || 0);
405:                     let jm = s.jm_obracun || s.jm_unos || 'm3';
406:                     let oznake = []; let napravljeno = 0; let skica = null;
407: 
408:                     if (isFazni) {
409:                         const fazaZaOvuMasinu = (tehnologija[s.id] || []).find(f => f.masina?.toUpperCase() === header.masina?.toUpperCase());
410:                         if (!fazaZaOvuMasinu) return; 
411: 
412:                         dimenzije = fazaZaOvuMasinu.dimenzija || dimenzije;
413:                         naruceno = parseFloat(fazaZaOvuMasinu.kolicina || 0);
414:                         jm = fazaZaOvuMasinu.jm || jm;
415:                         oznake = fazaZaOvuMasinu.oznake || [];
416:                         napravljeno = parseFloat(s.napravljeno_po_fazama?.[header.masina] || 0);
417:                         skica = fazaZaOvuMasinu.skica_url || fazaZaOvuMasinu.dokument_url || s.skica_url || s.dokument_url;
418:                     } else {
419:                         napravljeno = parseFloat(s.napravljeno || 0);
420:                         skica = s.skica_url || s.dokument_url;
421:                     }
422: 
423:                     const v = parseFloat(katItem.visina) || 0; const sir = parseFloat(katItem.sirina) || 0; const d = parseFloat(katItem.duzina) || 0;
424:                     const vol1kom = (v > 0 && sir > 0 && d > 0) ? (v/100) * (sir/100) * (d/100) : 0;
425: 
426:                     mapiraneStavke.push({
427:                         id: s.id, sifra_proizvoda: s.sifra, naziv_proizvoda: s.naziv, jm: jm, naruceno: naruceno, 
428:                         napravljeno: napravljeno, dimenzije: dimenzije, vol1kom: vol1kom, predlozene_oznake: oznake,
429:                         isFazni: isFazni, skica_url: skica 
430:                     });
431:                 });
432:                 
433:                 if(mapiraneStavke.length === 0 && isFazni) {
434:                     prikaziDialog({ tip: 'upozorenje', naslov: 'Nema Faza', poruka: `Ovaj nalog ne sadrži stavke predviđene za mašinu: ${header.masina}`, onCancel: zatvoriDialog });
435:                 }
436:                 setRnStavke(mapiraneStavke);
437:             } else { prikaziDialog({ tip: 'greska', naslov: 'Greška', poruka: `Nalog ${val} nema stavki!`, onCancel: zatvoriDialog }); setRnStavke([]); setTipNaloga(''); }
438:         } else { prikaziDialog({ tip: 'greska', naslov: 'Greška', poruka: `Nalog ${val} ne postoji!`, onCancel: zatvoriDialog }); setRnStavke([]); setTipNaloga(''); }
439:     };
440: 
441:     const handleStavkaSelect = async (stavka) => {
442:         let deb = '', sir = '', duz = '';
443:         if (stavka.isFazni && stavka.dimenzije && stavka.dimenzije !== 'Nema u katalogu') {
444:             const parts = stavka.dimenzije.split(/x|\*/i);
445:             if (parts.length >= 3) { deb = parts[0].trim(); sir = parts[1].trim(); duz = parts[2].trim(); }
446:         } else {

449:         }
450:

452:             ...form, 
453:             naziv: stavka.naziv_proizvoda, 
454:             debljina: deb, sirina: sir, duzina: duz, 
455:             jm: 'kom', rn_jm: stavka.jm, rn_stavka_id: stavka.id, 
456:             naruceno: parseFloat(stavka.naruceno).toFixed(4), 
457:             napravljeno: parseFloat(stavka.napravljeno || 0).toFixed(4) 
458:         });
459:         if (stavka.predlozene_oznake && stavka.predlozene_oznake.length > 0) {
460:             setOdabraneOznake(stavka.predlozene_oznake);
461:         } else {
462:             setOdabraneOznake([]);
463:         }
464:         dimRefs.debljina.current?.focus();
465:     };
466: 
467:     const processUlaz = async (val) => {
468:         let id = val.toUpperCase().trim();
469:         
470:         const { data: checkR } = await supabase.from('paketi')
471:             .select('paket_id')
472:             .eq('paket_id', `${id}-R`)
473:             .gt('kolicina_final', 0)
474:             .maybeSingle();
475:            
476:         if (checkR) {
477:             id = `${id}-R`; 
478:         }
479: 
480:         if (activeUlazIds.includes(id)) { setUlazScan(''); return; }
481:         
482:         const { data, error } = await supabase.from('paketi').select('*').eq('paket_id', id).gt('kolicina_final', 0);
483:         if (error) { prikaziDialog({ tip: 'greska', naslov: 'Baza Greška', poruka: error.message, onCancel: zatvoriDialog }); setUlazScan(''); return; }
484: 
485:         if (data && data.length > 0) { 
486:             const paket = data[0];
487:             setActiveUlazIds(prev => [...prev, id]); 
488:             setUlazneStavke(prev => [...prev, ...data]);
489:             setUlazScan(''); 
490: 
491:             // Ako desna forma još nema odabran naziv, predloži naziv sa ulaznog paketa
492:             if (!form.naziv && paket.naziv_proizvoda) {
493:                 setForm(prev => ({ ...prev, naziv: paket.naziv_proizvoda }));
494:             }
495: 
496:             if (id.endsWith('-R')) {
497:                  toast.warning(`⚠️ PAŽNJA: Ovaj paket (${id}) je VRAĆENA REKLAMACIJA!`);
498:             }
499: 
500:             if (paket.broj_veze && !radniNalog) {
501:                 prikaziDialog({
502:                     tip: 'info', naslov: 'Vezani Nalog',
503:                     poruka: `Skenirani paket pripada Radnom Nalogu: ${paket.broj_veze}\nŽelite li automatski učitati taj nalog na mašinu?`,
504:                     confirmText: '✅ DA, UČITAJ', cancelText: '✕ NE',
505:                     onConfirm: () => { handleNalogSelect(paket.broj_veze); zatvoriDialog(); },
506:                     onCancel: zatvoriDialog
507:                 });
508:             }
509:         } else { 
510:             prikaziDialog({ tip: 'upozorenje', naslov: 'Paket ne postoji', poruka: `ULAZNI paket ${id} ne postoji u bazi, ili je njegova količina nula (potrošen)!`, onCancel: zatvoriDialog });
511:             setUlazScan(''); 
512:         }
513:     };
514: 
515:     const handleUlazInput = (val, isEnter = false) => {
516:         setUlazScan(val);
517:         if(ulazTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(ulazTimerRef.current);
518:         if(!val) return;
519:         if (isEnter) processUlaz(val);
520:         else ulazTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => processUlaz(val), 2000);
521:     };
522: 
523:     // 🟢 POPRAVLJENO RAZDUŽIVANJE ULAZNOG PAKETA: Ažurira i kolicina_final i komade, poštuje DB constraint i odmah zatvara modal uz potvrdu!
524:     const potvrdiRazduzivanje = async () => {
525:         if (!razduziKol || isNaN(razduziKol)) {
526:             return toast.warning("Unesite ispravnu količinu za razduživanje!");
527:         }
528:         const unos = parseFloat(razduziKol.toString().replace(',', '.'));
529:         if (unos <= 0) {
530:             return toast.error("Količina mora biti veća od 0!");
531:         }
532: 
533:         const v = parseFloat(razduziZapis.debljina) || 1;
534:         const s = parseFloat(razduziZapis.sirina) || 1;
535:         const d = parseFloat(razduziZapis.duzina) || 1;
536:         const vol1Kom = (v / 100) * (s / 100) * (d / 100);
537: 
538:         let unosM3 = unos;
539:         if (razduziJm === 'kom') unosM3 = unos * vol1Kom;
540:         else if (razduziJm === 'm2') unosM3 = unos * (v / 100);
541:         else if (razduziJm === 'm1') unosM3 = unos * (v / 100) * (s / 100);
542: 
543:         const trenutnoNaStanju = parseFloat(razduziZapis.kolicina_final || 0);
544:         if (razduziMod === 'potroseno' && unosM3 > trenutnoNaStanju + 0.0005) {
545:             return toast.error(`Ne možete potrošiti ${unosM3.toFixed(3)} m³ jer je na paketu dostupno ${trenutnoNaStanju.toFixed(3)} m³!`);
546:         }
547: 
548:         let preostalo = 0;
549:         if (razduziMod === 'potroseno') preostalo = trenutnoNaStanju - unosM3;
550:         else if (razduziMod === 'ostalo') preostalo = unosM3;
551:         if (preostalo < 0) preostalo = 0;
552: 
553:         const novaKolM3 = preostalo <= 0.0005 ? 0 : parseFloat(preostalo.toFixed(4));
554:         const noviKomadi = vol1Kom > 0 && novaKolM3 > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.round(novaKolM3 / vol1Kom)) : 1;
555: 
556:         const izvrsiRazduzivanje = async () => {
557:             if (novaKolM3 === 0) {
558:                 // Ako je paket 100% potrošen, označi ga kao razduženog u doradi (ili obriši ako nema izlazne veze) kako ne bi prekršio chk_paketi_pozitivni (kolicina_final > 0)
559:                 const { error } = await supabase
560:                     .from('paketi')
561:                     .update({
562:                         otpremnica_id: `POTROSENO-DORADA-${header?.datum || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}`,
563:                         status: 'POTROŠENO_U_DORADI',
564:                         closed_at: razduziZapis.closed_at || new Date().toISOString()
565:                     })
566:                     .eq('id', razduziZapis.id);
567: 
568:                 if (error) {
569:                     toast.error("Baza Greška: " + error.message);
570:                     return;
571:                 }
572:                 setUlazneStavke(prev => prev.filter(st => st.id !== razduziZapis.id));
573:                 toast.success(`✅ Paket ${razduziZapis.paket_id} je u potpunosti potrošen i razdužen!`);
574:             } else {
575:                 const { error } = await supabase
576:                     .from('paketi')
577:                     .update({
578:                         kolicina_final: novaKolM3,
579:                         kubikaza: novaKolM3,
580:                         kolicina_m3: novaKolM3,
581:                         kolicina_ulaz: noviKomadi,
582:                         komada: noviKomadi
583:                     })
584:                     .eq('id', razduziZapis.id);
585: 
586:                 if (error) {
587:                     toast.error("Baza Greška: " + error.message);
588:                     return;
589:                 }
590:                 setUlazneStavke(prev => prev.map(st => st.id === razduziZapis.id ? {
591:                     ...st,
592:                     kolicina_final: novaKolM3,
593:                     kolicina_ulaz: noviKomadi,
594:                     komada: noviKomadi
595:                 } : st));
596:                 toast.success(`✅ Razduženo ${unosM3.toFixed(3)} m³ sa paketa ${razduziZapis.paket_id}! Preostalo: ${novaKolM3.toFixed(3)} m³ (${noviKomadi} kom).`);
597:             }
598: 
599:             setRazduziZapis(null);
600:             setRazduziKol('');
601:             zatvoriDialog();
602:         };
603: 
604:         if (novaKolM3 === 0) {
605:             setRazduziZapis(null);
606:             prikaziDialog({
607:                 tip: 'upozorenje',
608:                 naslov: 'Potvrda Potpune Potrošnje',
609:                 poruka: `Stanje ove stavke (${razduziZapis.paket_id}) će pasti na NULU i biće potpuno razdužena sa ulaza.\nDa li ste sigurni?`,
610:                 confirmText: '✅ DA, RAZDUŽI',
611:                 cancelText: '✕ ODUSTANI',
612:                 onConfirm: izvrsiRazduzivanje,
613:                 onCancel: zatvoriDialog
614:             });
615:         } else {
616:             await izvrsiRazduzivanje();
617:         }
618:     };
619: 
620:     const ukloniIzAktivnihUlaza = (paket_id) => {
621:         setActiveUlazIds(prev => prev.filter(id => id !== paket_id));
622:         setUlazneStavke(prev => prev.filter(s => s.paket_id !== paket_id));
623:     };
624: 
625:     const processIzlaz = async (val) => {
626:         const id = val.toUpperCase().trim();
627:         if (!activeIzlazIds.includes(id)) {
628:             const { data: existing } = await supabase.from('paketi').select('*').eq('paket_id', id);
629:             if (existing && existing.length > 0) {
630:                 const spisak = existing.map(i => `- ${i.naziv_proizvoda}: ${i.kolicina_final} ${i.jm}`).join('\n');
631:                 return prikaziDialog({
632:                     tip: 'info', naslov: 'Paket Pronađen',
633:                     poruka: `📦 PAKET VEĆ POSTOJI: ${id}\n\nTrenutno sadrži:\n${spisak}\n\nDa li želite AŽURIRATI ovaj paket?`,
634:                     confirmText: '✅ AŽURIRAJ', cancelText: '✕ PONIŠTI',
635:                     onConfirm: () => { setActiveIzlazIds(p => [...p, id]); setSelectedIzlazId(id); fetchIzlaz(id); setIzlazScan(''); zatvoriDialog(); ucitajSveZapoocetePakete(); },
636:                     onCancel: () => { setIzlazScan(''); zatvoriDialog(); }
637:                 });
638:             }
639:             setActiveIzlazIds(p => [...p, id]);
640:             ucitajSveZapoocetePakete();
641:         }
642:         setSelectedIzlazId(id); fetchIzlaz(id); setIzlazScan('');
643:     };
644:     const handleIzlazInput = (val, isEnter = false) => {
645:         setIzlazScan(val);
646:         if (izlazTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(izlazTimerRef.current);
647:         if (!val) return;
648:         if (isEnter) processIzlaz(val);
649:         else izlazTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => processIzlaz(val), 2000);
650:     };
651:     const fetchIzlaz = async (pid) => { 
652:         const { data } = await supabase.from('paketi').select('*').eq('paket_id', pid).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
653:         setIzlazPackageItems(data || []); 
654:         if (data && data.length > 0 && data[0].broj_veze && !radniNalog) { handleNalogSelect(data[0].broj_veze); }
655:     };
656:     const toggleOznaka = (o) => { setOdabraneOznake(prev => prev.includes(o) ? prev.filter(x => x !== o) : [...prev, o]); };
657:     
658:     const saveIzlaz = async () => {
659:         if (!selectedIzlazId) return prikaziDialog({ tip: 'upozorenje', naslov: 'Fali Paket', poruka: "Prvo skenirajte IZLAZNI PAKET!", onCancel: zatvoriDialog });
660:         if (!form.kolicina_ulaz || parseFloat(form.kolicina_ulaz) <= 0) return prikaziDialog({ tip: 'upozorenje', naslov: 'Neispravna Količina', poruka: "Količina mora biti veća od nule (0)!", onCancel: zatvoriDialog });
661:         if (parseFloat(form.debljina) <= 0 || parseFloat(form.sirina) <= 0 || parseFloat(form.duzina) <= 0) return prikaziDialog({ tip: 'upozorenje', naslov: 'Neispravne Dimenzije', poruka: "Sve dimenzije (Debljina, Širina, Dužina u cm) moraju biti veće od nule!", onCancel: zatvoriDialog });
662:         const izvrsiProcesSnimanja = async () => {
663:             const timeNow = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('de-DE');
664:             const unosKol = parseFloat(form.kolicina_ulaz);
665:             const v = parseFloat(form.debljina) || 1; const s = parseFloat(form.sirina) || 1; const d = parseFloat(form.duzina) || 1;
666: 
667:             let komada = unosKol;
668:             if (form.jm === 'm3') komada = unosKol / ((v/100) * (s/100) * (d/100));
669:             else if (form.jm === 'm2') komada = unosKol / ((s/100) * (d/100));
670:             else if (form.jm === 'm1') komada = unosKol / (d/100);
671: 
672:             const qtyZaPaket = parseFloat((komada * (v/100) * (s/100) * (d/100)).toFixed(3));
673:             let isNusProizvod = false; let currentRnStavka = null;
674: 
675:             if (form.rn_stavka_id) {
676:                 currentRnStavka = rnStavke.find(rs => rs.id === form.rn_stavka_id);
677:                 if (currentRnStavka && currentRnStavka.naziv_proizvoda !== form.naziv) isNusProizvod = true; 
678:             } else if (radniNalog) { isNusProizvod = true;
679:             }
680: 
681:             if (currentRnStavka && !isNusProizvod && !activeEditItem && !activeUlazIds.includes(selectedIzlazId)) {
682:                 const vecNapravljeno = parseFloat(currentRnStavka.napravljeno) || 0;
683:                 const naruceno = parseFloat(currentRnStavka.naruceno) || 0;
684:                 
685:                 let kolikoSadPravimo = komada;
686:                 if (currentRnStavka.jm === 'm3') kolikoSadPravimo = qtyZaPaket;
687:                 else if (currentRnStavka.jm === 'm2') kolikoSadPravimo = komada * (s/100) * (d/100);
688:                 else if (currentRnStavka.jm === 'm1') kolikoSadPravimo = komada * (d/100);
689:                 
690:                 if ((vecNapravljeno + kolikoSadPravimo) > naruceno) {
691:                     const preostalo = naruceno - vecNapravljeno;
692:                     return prikaziDialog({
693:                         tip: 'upozorenje', naslov: 'Prelazite Normu!',
694:                         poruka: `Za ovaj Radni Nalog je preostalo samo: ${preostalo.toFixed(3)} ${currentRnStavka.jm}.\nVi pokušavate dodati: ${kolikoSadPravimo.toFixed(3)} ${currentRnStavka.jm}.\n\nDa li ste sigurni da želite napraviti ovaj višak?`,
695:                         confirmText: '✅ DA, NAPRAVI VIŠAK', cancelText: '✕ PREKINI',
696:                         onConfirm: () => { zavrsiSnimanjeUBazu(qtyZaPaket, isNusProizvod, currentRnStavka, komada, v, s, d); zatvoriDialog(); },
697:                         onCancel: zatvoriDialog
698:                     });
699:                 }
700:             }
701:             await zavrsiSnimanjeUBazu(qtyZaPaket, isNusProizvod, currentRnStavka, komada, v, s, d);
702:         };
703: 
704:         if (activeUlazIds.length === 0) {
705:             prikaziDialog({
706:                 tip: 'upozorenje', naslov: 'Nema Sirovine',
707:                 poruka: "Niste učitali nijedan ULAZNI paket (sirovinu). Da li sigurno želite snimiti izlaz bez porijekla?",
708:                 confirmText: '✅ DA, SNIMI', cancelText: '✕ ODUSTANI',
709:                 onConfirm: () => { zatvoriDialog(); izvrsiProcesSnimanja(); },
710:                 onCancel: zatvoriDialog
711:             });
712:         } else {
713:             await izvrsiProcesSnimanja();
714:         }
715:     };
716: 
717:     const zavrsiSnimanjeUBazu = async (qtyZaPaket, isNusProizvod, currentRnStavka, komada, v, s, d) => {
718:         const timeNow = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('de-DE');
719:         const { data: aktuelniRadnici } = await supabase.from('aktivni_radnici').select('radnik_ime').eq('masina_naziv', header.masina).is('vrijeme_odjave', null);
720:         const ostaliRadnici = aktuelniRadnici ? aktuelniRadnici.map(r => r.radnik_ime).filter(ime => ime !== operater && ime !== viljuskarista) : [];

1218:                                                         const preostalo = rnStavka.naruceno - rnStavka.napravljeno;

1220:                                                             let pM3 = 0, pKom = 0;
1221:                                                             if (rnStavka.jm === 'm3') { pM3 = preostalo; pKom = rnStavka.vol1kom > 0 ? Math.ceil(preostalo / rnStavka.vol1kom) : 0; }
1222:                                                             else if (rnStavka.jm === 'kom') { pKom = preostalo; pM3 = preostalo * rnStavka.vol1kom; }
1223:                                                             else { pM3 = preostalo; }
1224:                                                             preostaloText = `${pM3.toFixed(3)} m³`;
1225:                                                         } else preostaloText = '0 (GOTOVO)';
1226:                                                     }
1227: 
1228:                                                     return (
1229:                                                     <div key={item.id} onClick={() => { setActiveEditItem(item); setForm({...item, kolicina_ulaz: '' }); setOdabraneOznake(item.oznake || []); }} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between sm:items-center p-4 bg-theme-card border border-theme-border/50 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:border-emerald-500 transition-all shadow-sm group gap-3">
1230:                                                         <div className="flex-1">
1231:                                                             <div className="text-xs uppercase text-theme-text font-black">{item.naziv_proizvoda} {item.je_nusproizvod ? <span className="text-amber-500 text-[9px] ml-2">(Nusproizvod)</span> : ''}</div>
1232:                                                             <div className="text-emerald-500 text-lg font-black tracking-tighter mt-1">{item.debljina}x{item.sirina}x{item.duzina} <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 ml-1">cm</span></div>
1233:                                                             <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center mt-2">
1234:                                                                 {item.oznake && item.oznake.length > 0 && (<div className="flex gap-1">{item.oznake.map(o => <span key={o} className="text-[8px] bg-amber-900/40 text-amber-400 px-2 py-1 rounded uppercase font-black">{o}</span>)}</div>)}
1235:                                                                 {rnStavka && !item.je_nusproizvod && (<div className="text-[9px] bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-500/30 px-2 py-1 rounded uppercase font-black text-blue-400 shadow-sm">Nalog Preostalo: <span className={preostaloText !== '0 (GOTOVO)' ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-emerald-400'}>{preostaloText}</span></div>)}
1236:                                                             </div>
1237:                                                         </div>
1238:                                                         <div className="flex flex-col sm:items-end gap-3 sm:border-l sm:border-theme-border sm:pl-5 w-full sm:w-auto">
1239:                                                             <div className="flex sm:flex-col justify-between sm:justify-end items-center sm:items-end w-full sm:w-auto">

1243:                                                             <div className="flex gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">

1245:                                                                 <button onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); printDeklaracijaPaketa(item.paket_id, [item], radniNalog); }} className="flex-1 sm:flex-none bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-400 hover:bg-blue-600 hover:text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[9px] font-black uppercase opacity-100 sm:opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all border border-blue-500/30 text-center">🖨️</button>
1246:                                                             </div>

1248:                                                     </div>

1250:                                             </div>
1251:                                         </div>
1252:                                     )}
1253:                                 </div>
1254:                             )}
1255:                         </div>
1256:                     </div>
1257:                 </div>

1261:                 modul="Dorada" 
1262:                 header={header} 
1263:                 user={user} 
1264:                 saas={saas} 
1265:                 updatePolje={updatePolje} 
1266:                 toggleVelicina={toggleVelicinaPolja}

8: const formatirajTacanDatum = (d1, d2, d3) => {
9:     const moguciDatumi = [d1, d2, d3].filter(Boolean);
10:     for (let dt of moguciDatumi) {
11:         try {
12:             const d = new Date(dt);
13:             if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) {
14:                 const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
15:                 const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
16:                 const yyyy = d.getFullYear();
17:                 const hh = String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
18:                 const min = String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
19:                 return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd} ${hh}:${min}`;
20:             }
21:         } catch(e) {}
22:     }
23:     return 'N/A';
24: };

29:         try { const d = new Date(dt); if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return d.getTime(); } catch(e) {}
30:     }
31:     return 0;
32: };

35:     if (val === null || val === undefined) return 'prazno';
36:     if (typeof val === 'object') return JSON.stringify(val);
37:     return String(val);
38: };

41: const renderStavkeDiff = (stareRaw, noveRaw) => {
42:     let stare = []; let nove = [];
43:     try { stare = typeof stareRaw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(stareRaw) : (stareRaw || []); } catch(e) { stare = stareRaw && typeof stareRaw === 'object' ? [stareRaw] : []; }
44:     try { nove = typeof noveRaw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(noveRaw) : (noveRaw || []); } catch(e) { nove = noveRaw && typeof noveRaw === 'object' ? [noveRaw] : []; }

46:     if (!Array.isArray(stare)) stare = [stare];
47:     if (!Array.isArray(nove)) nove = [nove];
48: 
49:     let subDiffs = [];

51:         ...stare.map((s, idx) => s.id || s.sifra || s.paket_id || `red_${idx}`),

59: 
60:         if (!o && n) {
61:             subDiffs.push(
62:                 <div key={`add-${kljuc}`} className="text-emerald-400 font-bold bg-emerald-950/30 p-2 rounded border border-emerald-500/20 text-[10px] my-1 break-all">
63:                     ➕ DODANA STAVKA: <span className="text-theme-text uppercase">{nazivArtikla}</span> ➔ {JSON.stringify(n)}
64:                 </div>
65:             );
66:         } else if (o && !n) {
67:             subDiffs.push(
68:                 <div key={`rem-${kljuc}`} className="text-red-400 font-bold bg-red-950/30 p-2 rounded border border-red-500/20 text-[10px] my-1 break-all">
69:                     ❌ UKLONJENA STAVKA: <span className="text-slate-400 line-through uppercase">{nazivArtikla}</span> ➔ {JSON.stringify(o)}
70:                 </div>
71:             );
72:         } else {
73:             const unutrašnjePromjene = [];
74:             const unutrašnjiKljučevi = Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(o), ...Object.keys(n)]));
75:             
76:             unutrašnjiKljučevi.forEach(sk => {
77:                 if (JSON.stringify(o[sk]) !== JSON.stringify(n[sk])) {
78:                     unutrašnjePromjene.push(
79:                         <div key={sk} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-1 text-[10px] ml-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-1 last:border-0">
80:                             <span className="text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase w-24 shrink-0">• {sk}:</span>
81:                             <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap min-w-0">
82:                                 <span className="text-red-400 line-through bg-red-950/40 px-1 rounded font-mono break-all">{siguranString(o[sk])}</span>
83:                                 <span className="text-slate-500">➔</span>
84:                                 <span className="text-emerald-400 font-black bg-emerald-900/40 px-1 rounded font-mono break-all">{siguranString(n[sk])}</span>
85:                             </div>
86:                         </div>
87:                     );
88:                 }
89:             });
90: 
91:             if (unutrašnjePromjene.length > 0) {
92:                 subDiffs.push(
93:                     <div key={`mod-${kljuc}`} className="bg-theme-panel p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-theme-border text-[10px] my-1">
94:                         <span className="text-amber-400 font-black uppercase block mb-1.5">✏️ Izmjena unutar stavke: {nazivArtikla}</span>

105: const PrikazIzmjena = ({ log }) => {
106:     const stari = log.stari_podaci || {};
107:     const novi = log.novi_podaci || {};
108: 
109:     const svaPoljaZaAnalizu = Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(stari), ...Object.keys(novi)]));
110:     let ispisSvihIzmjena = [];
111:

113:         const staroVal = stari[k];
114:         const novoVal = novi[k];
115: 
116:         if (JSON.stringify(staroVal) === JSON.stringify(novoVal)) return;
117: 
118:         if (typeof novoVal === 'object' || typeof staroVal === 'object' || k.includes('jsonb') || k.includes('ids') || Array.isArray(novoVal)) {
119:             const listaDiff = renderStavkeDiff(staroVal, novoVal);
120:             if (listaDiff) {
121:                 ispisSvihIzmjena.push(

124:                         {listaDiff}
125:                     </div>
126:                 );
127:             }
128:             return;
129:         }
130: 
131:         ispisSvihIzmjena.push(
132:             <div key={k} className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center gap-2 text-[11px] py-2 border-b border-theme-border/30 last:border-0 hover:bg-theme-panel/40 px-2 rounded transition-all">
133:                 <span className="text-slate-400 uppercase w-32 shrink-0 font-bold">• {k.replace(/_/g, ' ')}:</span>

138:                 </div>
139:             </div>
140:         );
141:     });
142: 
143:     if (ispisSvihIzmjena.length === 0) {
144:         return (
145:             <div className="mt-2 p-3 bg-theme-panel rounded-xl border border-theme-border shadow-inner">
146:                 <p className="text-[11px] text-theme-text opacity-80 leading-relaxed italic">{log.detalji}</p>
147:             </div>

234:         if (trupacBaza && trupacBaza.parent_id) {

269:             if (otpDb) {

287:             }
288:             if (otpremnicaIdZaLanac && !racunIdZaLanac) {
289:                 const { data: racDb } = await supabase.from('racuni').select('id').eq('broj_veze', otpremnicaIdZaLanac).maybeSingle();

291:             }
292:         }
293: 
294:         if (ponudaIdZaLanac && !nalogIdZaLanac) {
295:             const { data: rnDb } = await supabase.from('radni_nalozi').select('id').eq('broj_ponude', ponudaIdZaLanac).maybeSingle();
296:             if (rnDb) nalogIdZaLanac = rnDb.id;
297:         }
298: 
299:         let cistiIdovi = [val, nalogIdZaLanac, otpremnicaIdZaLanac, racunIdZaLanac, ponudaIdZaLanac].filter(Boolean);
300:

302:         const orConditions = cistiIdovi.map(id => `detalji.ilike.%${id}%,akcija.ilike.%${id}%`).join(',');
303:         const { data: sLogs } = await (orConditions ? supabase.from('sistem_audit_log').select('*').or(orConditions) : { data: [] });
304:         
305:         let sysLogsRaw = [];
306:         if (sLogs) {
307:             sysLogsRaw = sLogs.filter(l => {
308:                 const tekst = `${l.akcija || ''} ${l.detalji || ''} ${JSON.stringify(l.stari_podaci || '')} ${JSON.stringify(l.novi_podaci || '')}`.toUpperCase();
309:                 return cistiIdovi.some(cid => {
310:                     if (cid.length <= 4) {
311:                         const escaped = cid.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
312:                         const regex = new RegExp(`(^|[^0-9A-Z-])${escaped}([^0-9A-Z-]|$)`, 'i');
313:                         return regex.test(tekst);
314:                     }
315:                     return tekst.includes(cid);
316:                 });
317:             });
318: 
319:             // Ako je u audit logovima ovog dokumenta (npr. RAC-2026-352) zabilježena izvorna ponuda (npr. PON-2026-8578), poveži je u lanac!
320:             if (!ponudaIdZaLanac && (val.startsWith('RAC-') || val.startsWith('OTP-') || val.startsWith('RN-'))) {

323:                     if (m) {
324:                         ponudaIdZaLanac = m[0].toUpperCase();
325:                         if (!cistiIdovi.includes(ponudaIdZaLanac)) cistiIdovi.push(ponudaIdZaLanac);
326:                         break;
327:                     }
328:                 }
329:             }
330:         }
331:

338:             supabase.from('racuni').select('*').in('id', cistiIdovi),

340:         ]);

356:             fokusiraniPaketi.forEach(p => {

360:                 agregacija[kljuc].kolicina_final += finalDodato;
361:                 if (p.oznake) p.oznake.forEach(o => agregacija[kljuc].oznake.add(o));

367:             let trupciIds = []; let paketiIds = [];

370:                 if (p.ai_sirovina_ids) paketiIds.push(...(Array.isArray(p.ai_sirovina_ids) ? p.ai_sirovina_ids : String(p.ai_sirovina_ids).split(',').filter(Boolean)));
371:             });

373:                 const { data } = await supabase.from('trupci').select('*').in('id', [...new Set(trupciIds)]);

384:

389:         }
390:

399:                 const odstupanje = primijenjeniRabat - defaultRabat;

401:                     sifra: st.sifra, naziv: st.naziv, kolicina: st.kolicina_obracun, cijena_baza: st.cijena_baza,
402:                     sistemski_rabat: defaultRabat, primijenjeni_rabat: primijenjeniRabat, odstupanje: odstupanje,
403:                     upozorenje: odstupanje > 0 ? `⚠️ Ručno povećan rabat za ${odstupanje}%!` : `U skladu sa bazom`

406:         }
407: 
408:         const reklamacijskiLogovi = (rekPodaci || []).map(r => ({

420:         setForenzika(dataChain); setLoading(false); setSken(val);

14: function OTP_SearchableNalog({ nalozi, ponude, value, onChange, onScanClick }) {
15:     const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
16:     const [search, setSearch] = useState(value || '');
17:     const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] = useState(0);
18:     const wrapperRef = useRef(null);
19: 
20:     useEffect(() => { setSearch(value || ''); }, [value]);
21:     useEffect(() => { setSelectedIndex(0); }, [search]);
22: 
23:     useEffect(() => {
24:         function handleClickOutside(event) {
25:             if (wrapperRef.current && !wrapperRef.current.contains(event.target)) setOpen(false);
26:         }
27:         document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
28:         return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
29:     }, []);
30: 
31:     const kombinovanaLista = useMemo(() => {
32:         const rnList = (nalozi || []).map(n => ({ id: n.id, kupac_naziv: n.kupac_naziv, status: n.status, tip: 'RADNI NALOG' }));
33:         const ponList = (ponude || []).map(p => ({ id: p.id, kupac_naziv: p.kupac_naziv, status: p.status, tip: 'PONUDA' }));
34:         return [...rnList, ...ponList];
35:     }, [nalozi, ponude]);
36: 
37:     const q = (search || '').toString().toUpperCase();
38:     const filtered = kombinovanaLista.filter(item =>
39:         (item.id || '').toUpperCase().includes(q) ||
40:         (item.kupac_naziv || '').toUpperCase().includes(q)
41:     );
42: 
43:     const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
44:         if (!open) {
45:             if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') setOpen(true);
46:             else if (e.key === 'Enter' && search) { onChange(search, true); setOpen(false); }
47:             return;
48:         }
49:         if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
50:             e.preventDefault();
51:             setSelectedIndex(prev => (prev < filtered.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : prev));
52:         } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
53:             e.preventDefault();
54:             setSelectedIndex(prev => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : 0));
55:         } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
56:             e.preventDefault();
57:             if (filtered.length > 0 && filtered[selectedIndex]) {
58:                 const sel = filtered[selectedIndex];
59:                 onChange(sel.id, true);
60:                 setSearch(sel.id);
61:                 setOpen(false);
62:             } else if (search) {
63:                 onChange(search, true);
64:                 setOpen(false);
65:             }
66:         } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
67:             setOpen(false);
68:         }
69:     };
70: 
71:     return (
72:         <div ref={wrapperRef} className="relative font-black w-full flex bg-theme-panel border-2 border-orange-500 rounded-xl overflow-visible focus-within:border-orange-400 transition-all shadow-inner min-h-[45px]">
73:             <input
74:                 value={search}
75:                 onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
76:                 onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
77:                 onChange={e => { setSearch(e.target.value); setOpen(true); onChange(e.target.value, false); }}
78:                 onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
79:                 placeholder="Skeniraj ili upiši broj Radnog Naloga / Ponude..."
80:                 className="flex-1 min-w-0 p-4 bg-transparent text-sm text-theme-text outline-none uppercase font-black tracking-widest relative z-10"
81:             />
82:             <button onClick={onScanClick} className="shrink-0 px-6 bg-orange-600 text-white text-xl hover:bg-orange-500 transition-all border-l border-orange-500/50 relative z-10">📷</button>
83:             {open && search && (
84:                 <div className="absolute top-full left-0 z-[100] w-full mt-2 bg-theme-panel border border-theme-border rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-h-60 overflow-y-auto text-left custom-scrollbar">
85:                     {filtered.length === 0 && <div className="p-3 text-xs text-slate-500 text-center italic">Nema rezultata...</div>}
86:                     {filtered.map((item, idx) => (
87:                         <div
88:                             key={item.id}
89:                             onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onChange(item.id, true); setSearch(item.id); setOpen(false); }}
90:                             onMouseEnter={() => setSelectedIndex(idx)}
91:                             className={`p-3 border-b border-theme-border cursor-pointer transition-colors ${idx === selectedIndex ? 'bg-orange-600 text-white' : 'hover:bg-theme-card text-theme-text'}`}
92:                         >
93:                             <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
94:                                 <span className="text-sm font-black">{item.id}</span>
95:                                 <span className="text-[9px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/30 uppercase">{item.tip}</span>
96:                             </div>
97:                             <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-300 mt-1">{item.kupac_naziv} | Status: {item.status}</div>
98:                         </div>
99:                     ))}
100:                 </div>
101:             )}
102:         </div>
103:     );
104: }

229:                 supabase.from('katalog_proizvoda').select('*').order('sifra'),
230:                 supabase.from('radni_nalozi').select('*').neq('status', 'OBRISANO').order('datum', { ascending: false }),
231:                 supabase.from('ponude').select('*').neq('status', 'OBRISANO').order('datum', { ascending: false }),
232:                 supabase.from('otpremnice').select('*').order('datum', { ascending: false }),
233:                 supabase.from('paketi').select('*').not('closed_at', 'is', null).is('otpremnica_id', null).gt('kolicina_final', 0).order('created_at', { ascending: false })
234:             ]);

237:             setKatalog(catRes.data || []);

240:             const sveOtp = otpRes.data || [];

242:

244:             const siroviPaketi = pakRes.data || [];
245:             const mapaPaketa = {};
246:             siroviPaketi.forEach(p => {
247:                 const pid = p.paket_id;
248:                 if (!pid) return;
249:                 const deb = parseFloat(p.debljina || 0);
250:                 const sir = parseFloat(p.sirina || 0);
251:                 const duz = parseFloat(p.duzina || 0);
252:                 const vol1Kom = (deb > 0 && sir > 0 && duz > 0) ? (deb / 100) * (sir / 100) * (duz / 100) : 0;
253:                 const m3Red = parseFloat(p.kolicina_final || 0);
254:                 let komRed = parseFloat(p.kolicina_ulaz ?? p.komada ?? 0);
255:                 if ((p.jm || 'kom').toLowerCase() !== 'kom' && vol1Kom > 0) {
256:                     komRed = Math.round(m3Red / vol1Kom);
257:                 }
258: 
259:                 if (!mapaPaketa[pid]) {
260:                     mapaPaketa[pid] = {
261:                         ...p,
262:                         ukupno_m3: m3Red,
263:                         ukupno_kom: komRed,
264:                         redovi_baze: [p]
265:                     };
266:                 } else {
267:                     mapaPaketa[pid].ukupno_m3 += m3Red;
268:                     mapaPaketa[pid].ukupno_kom += komRed;
269:                     mapaPaketa[pid].redovi_baze.push(p);
270:                 }
271:             });
272:             setSlobodniPaketi(Object.values(mapaPaketa));
273: 
274:             // 🟢 AUTOMATSKA SANACIJA: Ako postoji isporučena otpremnica kojoj djelimični paket još nije umanjen (npr. OTP-2026-2567 za RUČNO-2026-7375), odmah ga uskladi!

278:                     const azuriraneStavke = [];

289:                                 await supabase.from('paketi').update({
