1534: function TabKategorijeBlagajne({ prikaziDialog, zatvoriDialog }) {
1535:     const [kategorije, setKategorije] = useState([]);
1536:     const [form, setForm] = useState({ tip: 'ULAZ', naziv: '' });
1537: 
1538:     useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
1539:     const load = async () => { const {data} = await supabase.from('blagajna_kategorije').select('*').order('tip'); setKategorije(data||[]); };
1540: 
1541:     const dodajKategoriju = async () => {
1542:         if(!form.naziv) return toast.warning("Unesite naziv kategorije!");
1543:         await supabase.from('blagajna_kategorije').insert([form]); 
1544:         toast.success("Kategorija blagajne dodana!");
1545:         setForm({...form, naziv: ''}); load();
1546:     };
1547: 
1548:     const obrisiKategoriju = (id, naziv) => {
1549:         prikaziDialog({
1550:             tip: 'upozorenje',
1551:             naslov: 'Brisanje Kategorije Blagajne',
1552:             poruka: `Da li ste sigurni da želite obrisati kategoriju "${naziv}"?`,
1553:             confirmText: '🗑️ OBRIŠI',
1554:             cancelText: '✕ ODUSTANI',
1555:             onConfirm: async () => {
1556:                 await supabase.from('blagajna_kategorije').delete().eq('id', id);
1557:                 zatvoriDialog();
1558:                 toast.info(`Kategorija "${naziv}" obrisana.`);
1559:                 load();
1560:             },
1561:             onCancel: zatvoriDialog
1562:         });
1563:     };
1564: 
1565:     return (
1566:         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 animate-in fade-in max-w-5xl mx-auto">
1567:             <div className="bg-theme-card backdrop-blur-[var(--glass-blur)] p-6 rounded-box border border-theme-border shadow-2xl space-y-6">
1568:                 <h3 className="text-emerald-400 font-black uppercase text-xs">🗂️ Dodaj Kategoriju (Blagajna)</h3>
1569:                 <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
1570:                     <select value={form.tip} onChange={e=>setForm({...form, tip:e.target.value})} className="p-4 bg-theme-panel rounded-xl text-theme-text font-black outline-none border border-theme-border cursor-pointer uppercase text-sm focus:border-emerald-500"><option value="ULAZ">📈 ULAZ (Prihodi)</option><option value="IZLAZ">📉 IZLAZ (Troškovi)</option></select>
1571:                     <input value={form.naziv} onChange={e=>setForm({...form, naziv:e.target.value})} onKeyDown={e=>{if(e.key==='Enter') dodajKategoriju()}} placeholder="Npr. Kupovina alata..." className="w-full min-w-0 p-4 bg-theme-panel rounded-xl text-theme-text outline-none border border-theme-border text-sm font-bold focus:border-emerald-500" />
1572:                     <button onClick={dodajKategoriju} className="w-full bg-emerald-600 py-4 rounded-xl text-white font-black hover:bg-emerald-500 shadow-lg uppercase text-xs">➕ Dodaj Kategoriju</button>
1573:                 </div>
1574:             </div>
1575:             
1576:             <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6">
1577:                 {['ULAZ', 'IZLAZ'].map(tip => (
1578:                     <div key={tip} className="bg-theme-card p-6 rounded-box border border-theme-border shadow-xl">
1579:                         <h4 className={`text-xs uppercase font-black mb-4 border-b border-theme-border pb-2 ${tip==='ULAZ'?'text-emerald-500':'text-red-500'}`}>Kategorije za {tip}</h4>
1580:                         <div className="space-y-2 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto pr-2 custom-scrollbar">
1581:                             {kategorije.filter(k=>k.tip===tip).map(k => (
1582:                                 <div key={k.id} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 bg-theme-panel rounded-xl border border-theme-border hover:border-slate-500 transition-colors shadow-sm">
1583:                                     <span className="text-xs text-theme-text font-bold uppercase">{k.naziv}</span>
1584:                                     <button onClick={()=>obrisiKategoriju(k.id, k.naziv)} className="text-red-400 bg-red-900/20 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-black hover:text-white hover:bg-red-500 transition-all text-[10px] uppercase">✕ Obriši</button>
1585:                                 </div>
1586:                             ))}
1587:                             {kategorije.filter(k=>k.tip===tip).length === 0 && <p className="text-center text-xs text-slate-500 italic py-4">Nema kategorija.</p>}
1588:                         </div>
1589:                     </div>
1590:                 ))}
1591:             </div>
1592:         </div>
1593:     );
1594: }

1596: function TabBrending({ prikaziDialog, zatvoriDialog }) {
1597:     const [logotipi, setLogotipi] = useState([]);
1598:     const [form, setForm] = useState({ id: null, naziv: '', url_slike: '', lokacije_jsonb: [], full_width: false });
1599:     const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
1600:     const [fajlSlike, setFajlSlike] = useState(null);
1601:     const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
1602: 
1603:     const [firmaInfo, setFirmaInfo] = useState({ 
1604:         adresa: '', telefon: '', email: '', 
1605:         footer_tekst: '', footer_boja: '#64748b', footer_velicina: '12',
1606:         app_name: 'SmartERP'
1607:     });
1608: 
1609:     const [cloudPostavke, setCloudPostavke] = useState({
1610:         folder_prorez: '', folder_dorada: '', folder_ukupno: '', folder_finansije: '', webhook_url: ''
1611:     });
1612: 
1613:     const opcijeLokacija = ['Ikona u pregledniku (Favicon)', 'GLAVNI MENI / SIDEBAR (GORE LIJEVO)', 'Svi PDF Dokumenti', 'PDF Ponuda', 'PDF Radni Nalog', 'PDF Otpremnica', 'PDF Račun', 'PDF Blagajna'];
1614: 
1615:     useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
1616:     
1617:     const load = async () => { 
1618:         const { data } = await supabase.from('brending').select('*').order('naziv'); 
1619:         setLogotipi(data || []); 
1620:         
1621:         const { data: fData } = await supabase.from('postavke_firme').select('*').eq('id', 1).maybeSingle();
1622:         if (fData) {
1623:             setFirmaInfo({
1624:                 adresa: fData.adresa || '', telefon: fData.telefon || '', email: fData.email || '', 
1625:                 footer_tekst: fData.footer_tekst || '', footer_boja: fData.footer_boja || '#64748b', footer_velicina: fData.footer_velicina || '12',
1626:                 app_name: fData.app_name || localStorage.getItem('saas_app_name') || 'SmartERP'
1627:             });
1628:         } else {
1629:             setFirmaInfo(prev => ({...prev, app_name: localStorage.getItem('saas_app_name') || 'SmartERP'}));
1630:         }
1631: 
1632:         const { data: cData } = await supabase.from('postavke_izvjestaja').select('*').eq('id', 1).maybeSingle();
1633:         if (cData) setCloudPostavke(cData);
1634:     };
1635: 
1636:     const spasiFirmuInfo = async () => {
1637:         localStorage.setItem('saas_app_name', firmaInfo.app_name);
1638:         window.dispatchEvent(new Event('saas_updated'));
1639:         const { error } = await supabase.from('postavke_firme').upsert({ id: 1, app_name: firmaInfo.app_name, adresa: firmaInfo.adresa, telefon: firmaInfo.telefon, email: firmaInfo.email, footer_tekst: firmaInfo.footer_tekst, footer_boja: firmaInfo.footer_boja, footer_velicina: firmaInfo.footer_velicina });
1640:         if (error) toast.error("Greška pri spašavanju baze: " + error.message);
1641:         else toast.success("✅ Podaci o firmi i Naziv Aplikacije uspješno spašeni!");
1642:     };
1643: 
1644:     const spasiCloudPostavke = async () => {
1645:         const { error } = await supabase.from('postavke_izvjestaja').upsert({ id: 1, ...cloudPostavke });
1646:         if (error) toast.error("Greška pri spašavanju cloud postavki: " + error.message);
1647:         else toast.success("☁️ Cloud postavke za štampanje uspješno spašene!");
1648:     };
1649: 
1650:     const toggleLokacija = (lok) => { setForm(prev => ({...prev, lokacije_jsonb: prev.lokacije_jsonb.includes(lok) ? prev.lokacije_jsonb.filter(l => l !== lok) : [...prev.lokacije_jsonb, lok] })); };
1651:     const pokreniIzmjenu = (l) => { setForm({ id: l.id, naziv: l.naziv, url_slike: l.url_slike, lokacije_jsonb: l.lokacije_jsonb || [], full_width: l.full_width || false }); setFajlSlike(null); setIsEditing(true); window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); };
1652:     const ponisti = () => { setForm({ id: null, naziv: '', url_slike: '', lokacije_jsonb: [], full_width: false }); setFajlSlike(null); setIsEditing(false); };
1653:     
1654:     const uploadISnimi = async () => {
1655:         if(!form.naziv) return toast.warning("Naziv logotipa je obavezan!");
1656:         if(!form.url_slike && !fajlSlike) return toast.warning("Odaberite sliku sa računara ili unesite link!");
1657:         setUploading(true);
1658:         let finalUrl = form.url_slike;
1659: 
1660:         if (fajlSlike) {
1661:             const fileExt = fajlSlike.name.split('.').pop();
1662:             const fileName = `logo_${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`; 
1663:             const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage.from('slike').upload(fileName, fajlSlike);
1664:             if (uploadError) { setUploading(false); return toast.error("Greška pri uploadu: " + uploadError.message); }
1665:             const { data: publicUrlData } = supabase.storage.from('slike').getPublicUrl(fileName);
1666:             finalUrl = publicUrlData.publicUrl;
1667:         }
1668: 
1669:         const payload = { naziv: form.naziv, url_slike: finalUrl, lokacije_jsonb: form.lokacije_jsonb, full_width: form.full_width };
1670:         if(isEditing) await supabase.from('brending').update(payload).eq('id', form.id);
1671:         else await supabase.from('brending').insert([payload]);
1672:         
1673:         if (form.lokacije_jsonb.includes('GLAVNI MENI / SIDEBAR (GORE LIJEVO)')) {
1674:             localStorage.setItem('saas_app_logo', finalUrl);
1675:         } else {
1676:             if (localStorage.getItem('saas_app_logo') === finalUrl) {
1677:                 localStorage.setItem('saas_app_logo', '');
1678:             }
1679:         }
1680:         window.dispatchEvent(new Event('saas_updated'));
1681: 
1682:         setUploading(false); ponisti(); load();
1683:         toast.success("✅ Brending uspješno snimljen!");
1684:     };
1685: 
1686:     const obrisi = (id, naziv, slikaUrl, e) => { 
1687:         e.stopPropagation(); 
1688:         prikaziDialog({
1689:             tip: 'upozorenje',
1690:             naslov: 'Brisanje Logotipa',
1691:             poruka: `Da li ste sigurni da želite trajno obrisati logo: ${naziv}?`,
1692:             confirmText: '🗑️ OBRIŠI LOGO',
1693:             cancelText: '✕ ODUSTANI',
1694:             onConfirm: async () => {
1695:                 await supabase.from('brending').delete().eq('id', id); 
1696:                 if (localStorage.getItem('saas_app_logo') === slikaUrl) {
1697:                     localStorage.setItem('saas_app_logo', '');
1698:                     window.dispatchEvent(new Event('saas_updated'));
1699:                 }
1700:                 zatvoriDialog();
1701:                 toast.info(`Logo ${naziv} obrisan.`);
1702:                 load(); 
1703:             },
1704:             onCancel: zatvoriDialog
1705:         });
1706:     };
1707: 
1708:     return (
1709:         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 animate-in fade-in">
1710:             <div className="space-y-6">
1711:                 <div className="bg-theme-card backdrop-blur-[var(--glass-blur)] p-6 rounded-box border border-blue-500/30 shadow-2xl space-y-6">
1712:                     <div>
1713:                         <h3 className="text-blue-500 font-black uppercase text-xs border-b border-theme-border pb-2 mb-4">🖥️ Naziv Aplikacije (Glavni Meni / Sidebar)</h3>
1714:                         <label className="text-[8px] text-slate-500 uppercase ml-2 block mb-1">Tekst koji se prikazuje ako nema logotipa (Gore Lijevo)</label>
1715:                         <input value={firmaInfo.app_name} onChange={e=>setFirmaInfo({...firmaInfo, app_name:e.target.value})} onKeyDown={e=>{if(e.key==='Enter') spasiFirmuInfo()}} className="w-full min-w-0 p-4 bg-theme-panel rounded-xl text-lg text-theme-text font-black outline-none border border-theme-border focus:border-blue-500 uppercase tracking-widest shadow-inner" placeholder="Npr. Pilana ERP..." />
1716:                     </div>
1717: 
1718:                     <div className="pt-4 border-t border-theme-border">
1719:                         <h3 className="text-blue-500 font-black uppercase text-xs border-b border-theme-border pb-2 mb-4">🏢 Podaci o firmi (Za PDF Izvještaje)</h3>
1720:                         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4">

1894: export default function SettingsModule({ onExit, lockedTab }) {
1895:     const [tab, setTab] = useState(lockedTab || 'sumarije');
1896:     const [dialog, setDialog] = useState({ isOpen: false });
1897:     const prikaziDialog = (opcije) => setDialog({ isOpen: true, confirmText: 'POTVRDI', cancelText: 'ZATVORI', ...opcije });
1898:     const zatvoriDialog = () => setDialog({ isOpen: false });
1899: 
1900:     const tabs = ['sumarije', 'prevoznici', 'masine', 'katalog', 'kupci', 'radnici', 'blagajna', 'brending'];
1901:     const labels = { sumarije: '🌲 Šumarije', prevoznici: '🚚 Prevoznici', masine: '⚙️ Mašine', katalog: '📦 Katalog', kupci: '🤝 Kupci', radnici: '👷 Radnici', blagajna: '🗂️ Kase / Kat.', brending: '🎨 Brending / Print' };
1902: 
1903:     return (
1904:         <div className="p-4 max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-6">
1905:             <PametniDialog {...dialog} />
1906:             <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center bg-theme-card backdrop-blur-[var(--glass-blur)] p-4 rounded-[2rem] border border-theme-border shadow-xl gap-4">
1907:                 <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
1908:                     <button onClick={onExit} className={`text-[10px] px-5 py-3 rounded-xl uppercase font-black text-white transition-all shadow-md ${lockedTab ? 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500' : 'bg-theme-panel text-theme-text border border-theme-border hover:bg-slate-700 hover:text-white'}`}>
1909:                         {lockedTab ? '✕ ZATVORI I NASTAVI RAD' : '← Nazad u Meni'}
1910:                     </button>
1911:                     <h2 className={`${lockedTab ? 'text-amber-500' : 'text-slate-400'} font-black tracking-widest uppercase text-xs md:text-sm hidden md:block`}>
1912:                         {lockedTab ? '⚡ BRZI UNOS PODATAKA' : '⚙️ ERP CENTRALNE POSTAVKE'}
1913:                     </h2>
1914:                 </div>
1915:                 
1916:                 {!lockedTab && (
1917:                     <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 justify-center bg-theme-panel p-2 rounded-2xl border border-theme-border shadow-inner">
1918:                         {tabs.map(t => (
1919:                             <button key={t} onClick={() => setTab(t)} className={`px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all text-[9px] tracking-widest uppercase font-black ${tab === t ? 'bg-theme-accent border border-blue-400 text-white shadow-md' : 'bg-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-theme-text hover:bg-theme-card'}`}>
1920:                                 {labels[t]}
1921:                             </button>
1922:                         ))}
1923:                     </div>
1924:                 )}
1925:             </div>
1926:             
1927:             {tab === 'sumarije' && <TabSumarije prikaziDialog={prikaziDialog} zatvoriDialog={zatvoriDialog} />}
1928:             {tab === 'prevoznici' && <TabPrevoznici prikaziDialog={prikaziDialog} zatvoriDialog={zatvoriDialog} />}
1929:             {tab === 'masine' && <TabMasine prikaziDialog={prikaziDialog} zatvoriDialog={zatvoriDialog} />}
1930:             {tab === 'katalog' && <TabKatalog prikaziDialog={prikaziDialog} zatvoriDialog={zatvoriDialog} />}
1931:             {tab === 'kupci' && <TabKupci prikaziDialog={prikaziDialog} zatvoriDialog={zatvoriDialog} />}
1932:             {tab === 'radnici' && <TabRadnici prikaziDialog={prikaziDialog} zatvoriDialog={zatvoriDialog} />}
1933:             {tab === 'blagajna' && <TabKategorijeBlagajne prikaziDialog={prikaziDialog} zatvoriDialog={zatvoriDialog} />}
1934:             {tab === 'brending' && <TabBrending prikaziDialog={prikaziDialog} zatvoriDialog={zatvoriDialog} />}
1935:         </div>
1936:     );
1937: }

100:         login_naslov_1: "Smart",
101:         login_naslov_2: "Timber",

134:     const ucitajDashboardPodatke = async () => {
135:         setDashLoading(true);
136:         const danas = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
137:         const sedamDanaNazad = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0];
138:         try {
139:             const [trupciRes, paketiRes, racuniRes, naloziRes, feedRes, trendRes, trupciLagerRes, paketiLagerRes] = await Promise.all([
140:                 supabase.from('trupci').select('zapremina').eq('datum_prijema', danas),
141:                 supabase.from('paketi').select('kolicina_final').eq('datum_yyyy_mm', danas),
142:                 supabase.from('racuni').select('*').neq('status', 'NAPLAĆENO'), 
143:                 supabase.from('radni_nalozi').select('*').neq('status', 'ISPORUČENO'), 
144:                 supabase.from('sistem_audit_log').select('*').order('vrijeme', { ascending: false }).limit(8),
145:                 supabase.from('paketi').select('datum_yyyy_mm, kolicina_final').gte('datum_yyyy_mm', sedamDanaNazad).lte('datum_yyyy_mm', danas),
146:                 supabase.from('trupci').select('duzina, zapremina').eq('status', 'na_lageru').eq('zakljucen_prijem', true),
147:                 supabase.from('paketi').select('broj_veze, naziv_proizvoda, kolicina_final').not('closed_at', 'is', null).is('otpremnica_id', null)
148:             ]);
149:             const sumUlaz = (trupciRes.data || []).reduce((a,b)=>a+parseFloat(b.zapremina||0), 0);
150:             const sumIzlaz = (paketiRes.data || []).reduce((a,b)=>a+parseFloat(b.kolicina_final||0), 0);
151:             
152:             const sviRacuni = racuniRes.data || [];
153:             const sumFakturisano = sviRacuni.reduce((a,b)=>a+parseFloat(b.ukupno_sa_pdv||0), 0);
154:             
155:             const kupciDug = {};
156:             sviRacuni.forEach(r => { kupciDug[r.kupac_naziv] = (kupciDug[r.kupac_naziv] || 0) + parseFloat(r.ukupno_sa_pdv || 0); });
157:             const topDuznici = Object.keys(kupciDug).map(k => ({ name: k, dug: parseFloat(kupciDug[k].toFixed(2)) })).sort((a,b) => b.dug - a.dug).slice(0, 5);
158: 
159:             const trendMap = {};
160:             for(let i=6; i>=0; i--) { const d = new Date(Date.now() - i * 86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0];
161:             trendMap[d] = { dan: d.substring(5,10).replace('-', '.'), m3: 0 }; }
162:             (trendRes.data || []).forEach(p => { if(trendMap[p.datum_yyyy_mm]) trendMap[p.datum_yyyy_mm].m3 += parseFloat(p.kolicina_final||0); });
163:             const trupciMap = {};
164:             (trupciLagerRes.data || []).forEach(t => { const l = parseFloat(t.duzina).toFixed(1); trupciMap[l] = (trupciMap[l] || 0) + parseFloat(t.zapremina || 0); });
165:             const trupciChart = Object.keys(trupciMap).map(k => ({ duzina: k + 'm', m3: parseFloat(trupciMap[k].toFixed(2)) })).sort((a,b) => parseFloat(a.duzina) - parseFloat(b.duzina));
166:             const paketiMap = {};
167:             (paketiLagerRes.data || []).forEach(p => { const n = p.naziv_proizvoda; paketiMap[n] = (paketiMap[n] || 0) + parseFloat(p.kolicina_final || 0); });
168:             const paketiChart = Object.keys(paketiMap).map(k => ({ naziv: k, m3: parseFloat(paketiMap[k].toFixed(2)) })).sort((a,b) => b.m3 - a.m3).slice(0, 8);
169:             const sviNalozi = naloziRes.data || [];
170:             
171:             const naloziCekanje = sviNalozi.filter(n => n.status !== 'U PROIZVODNJI' && n.status !== 'ZAVRŠENO').map(n => ({
172:                 id: n.id, kupac: n.kupac_naziv, rok: n.rok_isporuke, status: n.status, stavki: (n.stavke_jsonb||[]).length
173:             })).sort((a,b) => new Date(a.rok) - new Date(b.rok));
174:             const naloziProgres = sviNalozi.filter(n => n.status === 'U PROIZVODNJI').map(n => {
175:                 const stavke = n.stavke_jsonb || [];
176:                 const cilj = stavke.reduce((s, st) => s + parseFloat(st.kolicina_obracun || st.kolicina_unos || 0), 0);
177:                 const uradjeno = stavke.reduce((s, st) => s + parseFloat(st.napravljeno || 0), 0);
178:              
179:                 const procenat = cilj > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((uradjeno / cilj) * 100)) : 0;
180:                 return { id: n.id, kupac: n.kupac_naziv, cilj: cilj.toFixed(2), uradjeno: uradjeno.toFixed(2), procenat };
181:             }).sort((a,b) => b.procenat - a.procenat);
182:             const spremnoMap = {};
183:             (paketiLagerRes.data || []).filter(p => p.broj_veze).forEach(p => {
184:                 spremnoMap[p.broj_veze] = (spremnoMap[p.broj_veze] || 0) + parseFloat(p.kolicina_final || 0);
185:             });
186:             const spremnoZaIsporuku = Object.keys(spremnoMap).map(rn => {
187:                 const nalogInfo = sviNalozi.find(n => n.id === rn);
188:                 return { id: rn, kupac: nalogInfo ? nalogInfo.kupac_naziv : 'Nepoznat Kupac', kolicina: spremnoMap[rn].toFixed(2) };
189:             }).sort((a,b) => b.kolicina - a.kolicina);
190:             setDashboardData({
191:                 kpi: { ulaz: sumUlaz.toFixed(1), izlaz: sumIzlaz.toFixed(1), potrazivanja: sumFakturisano.toLocaleString('bs-BA') },
192:                 feed: feedRes.data || [], trend: Object.values(trendMap), trupciChart, paketiChart, naloziCekanje, naloziProgres, spremnoZaIsporuku, topDuznici
193:             });
194:         } catch(e) {}
195:         setDashLoading(false);
196:     };

199:

201:     const odustaniOdEdita = () => { if (backupPostavke) setUiPostavke(backupPostavke); setIsEditMode(false); };

222:         setUiPostavke({ ...uiPostavke, widget_layout: novaLista });
223:     };

234:         setUiPostavke({ ...uiPostavke, widget_layout: novaLista });

295:             .from('ui_postavke')
296:             .upsert([{ modul_ime: 'dashboard', postavke_jsonb: uiPostavke }], { onConflict: 'modul_ime' });
297:

307:     useEffect(() => {
308:         const initApp = async () => {
309:             const userJson = localStorage.getItem('smart_timber_user');
310:             if (userJson) { try { setLoggedUser(JSON.parse(userJson)); } catch (e) { localStorage.removeItem('smart_timber_user'); } }
311:             try {
312:                 const [uiRes, brendRes] = await Promise.all([
313:                     supabase.from('ui_postavke').select('postavke_jsonb').eq('modul_ime', 'dashboard').maybeSingle(),
314:                     supabase.from('brending').select('*')
315:                 ]);
316: 
317:                 if (brendRes.data && brendRes.data.length > 0) {
318:                     let logoObj = brendRes.data.find(b => (b.lokacije_jsonb || []).includes('GLAVNI MENI / SIDEBAR (GORE LIJEVO)')) || brendRes.data[0];
319:                     if (logoObj?.url_slike) {
320:                         setLoginLogoUrl(logoObj.url_slike);
321:                         localStorage.setItem('saas_app_logo', logoObj.url_slike);
322:                     }
323:                 } else {
324:                     const cachedLogo = localStorage.getItem('saas_app_logo');
325:                     if (cachedLogo) setLoginLogoUrl(cachedLogo);
326:                 }
327: 
328:                 // Prvo gledamo bazu, a ako baza nema zapis, čitamo iz localStorage bekapa
329:                 let localBackup = null;
330:                 try {
331:                     const rawBackup = localStorage.getItem('erp_dashboard_ui_backup');
332:                     if (rawBackup) localBackup = JSON.parse(rawBackup);
333:                 } catch (e) {}
334: 
335:                 let fetchedSettings = uiRes.data?.postavke_jsonb || localBackup || { 
336:                     glavni_naslov: "Operativni Centar", 
337:                     pozdravna_poruka: "Odaberi modul — isti tok rada, novi izgled.", 
338:                     moduli: [], 
339:                     menu_style: {},
340:                     widget_layout: defaultWidgetLayout 
341:                 };
342:                 
343:                 if (!fetchedSettings.widget_layout) fetchedSettings.widget_layout = defaultWidgetLayout;
344:                 if (fetchedSettings.login_naslov_1 === undefined) fetchedSettings.login_naslov_1 = "Smart";
345:                 if (fetchedSettings.login_naslov_2 === undefined) fetchedSettings.login_naslov_2 = "Timber";
346:                 if (!fetchedSettings.login_user_label) fetchedSettings.login_user_label = "Username";
347:                 if (!fetchedSettings.login_pass_label) fetchedSettings.login_pass_label = "Password";
348:                 if (!fetchedSettings.login_btn_tekst) fetchedSettings.login_btn_tekst = "Pristupi sistemu";
349:                 if (!fetchedSettings.login_boja_akcenta) fetchedSettings.login_boja_akcenta = "#2563eb";
350:                 if (!Array.isArray(fetchedSettings.moduli)) fetchedSettings.moduli = [];
351: 
352:                 let changed = false;
353:                 defaultModuli.forEach(dm => {
354:                     if (!fetchedSettings.moduli.some(m => m.id === dm.id)) { 
355:                         fetchedSettings.moduli.push(dm); 
356:                         changed = true; 
357:                     }
358:                 });
359: 
360:                 setUiPostavke(fetchedSettings);
361:                 localStorage.setItem('erp_dashboard_ui_backup', JSON.stringify(fetchedSettings));
362: 
363:                 if (changed || !uiRes.data) {
364:                     supabase.from('ui_postavke').upsert([{ modul_ime: 'dashboard', postavke_jsonb: fetchedSettings }], { onConflict: 'modul_ime' }).then();
365:                 }
366:             } catch(e) {}
367:             setAuthLoading(false);
368:         };
369:         initApp();
370:     }, []);
371:     
372:     const handleLogin = async (e) => {
373:         e.preventDefault();
374:         const username = e.target.user.value.trim();
375:         const password = e.target.pass.value.trim();
376:         const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('provjeri_login', { uneseni_user: username, unesena_sifra: password });
377:         if (error) { toast.error("Greška pri komunikaciji sa serverom!"); } 
378:         else if (data) { localStorage.setItem('smart_timber_user', JSON.stringify(data)); setLoggedUser(data); toast.success(`Dobrodošli nazad, ${data.ime_prezime}!`); } 
379:         else { toast.error("Pogrešan Username ili Password!"); }
380:     };
381: 
382:     const hasPermission = (modulId) => {
383:         if (!loggedUser) return false;
384:         if (modulId === 'nadzor') return loggedUser.uloga === 'superadmin';
385:         if (loggedUser.uloga === 'superadmin' || loggedUser.uloga === 'admin') return true;
386:         const mapaDozvola = {
387:             'prijem': 'Prijem trupaca', 'prorez': 'Prorez (Trupci)', 'pilana': 'Pilana (Izlaz)', 'dorada': 'Dorada (Ulaz/Izlaz)',

678:         localStorage.setItem('erp_dashboard_ui_backup', JSON.stringify(novePostavke));
679:         const { error } = await supabase
680:             .from('ui_postavke')
681:             .upsert([{ modul_ime: 'dashboard', postavke_jsonb: novePostavke }], { onConflict: 'modul_ime' });
682:         if (error) {
683:             toast.error("Greška pri snimanju u bazu: " + error.message);
684:         }
685:     };
686: 
687:     return (
688:         <AppShell 
689:             user={loggedUser} 
690:             activeModule={activeModule} 
691:             onModuleChange={setActiveModule} 
692:             accentColor={modulBoja} 
693:             dynamicModules={finalniModuliZaMeni}
694:             menuConfig={uiPostavke.menu_style || {}}
695:             onSaveMenuConfig={handleSaveMenuFromShell}
696:         >
697:             <PametniDialog {...dialog} />
698:             
699:             {/* ACTION HUB / KONTEKSTNI MENI MODAL */}
700:             {kontekstniMeni && (
701:                 <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in">
702:                     <div className="bg-theme-card border-2 border-theme-accent p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(var(--theme-accent-rgb),0.3)] max-w-sm w-full relative">
703:                         <button onClick={() => setKontekstniMeni(null)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-slate-400 hover:text-white bg-slate-800 w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-colors">✕</button>
704:                         <h3 className="text-theme-accent font-black uppercase text-[10px] tracking-widest mb-1">{kontekstniMeni.title}</h3>
705:                         <p className="text-2xl text-white font-black">{kontekstniMeni.id}</p>
706:                         <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-bold mb-6">{kontekstniMeni.subtitle}</p>
707: 
708:                         <div className="space-y-3">
709:                             {kontekstniMeni.actions.map(akcija => {
710:                                 if (!hasPermission(akcija.module)) return null;
711:                                 return (
712:                                     <button 
713:                                         key={akcija.module} 
714:                                         onClick={() => handleDeepLink(akcija.module, kontekstniMeni.id, akcija.action)} 
715:                                         className="w-full py-4 bg-theme-panel hover:bg-theme-accent hover:text-white border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm"
716:                                     >
717:                                         {akcija.label}
718:                                     </button>
719:                                 );
720:                             })}
721:                             <button onClick={() => { navigator.clipboard.writeText(kontekstniMeni.id); toast.success("Kopirano!"); setKontekstniMeni(null); }} className="w-full py-3 bg-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase transition-all mt-2">📋 Kopiraj ID</button>
722:                         </div>
723:                     </div>
724:                 </div>
725:             )}
726: 
727:             <div className="w-full min-h-full transition-colors duration-700 font-sans">
728:                 {activeModule === 'home' ? (
729:                     <div className="p-4 md:p-8 selection:bg-emerald-500/30 w-full animate-in fade-in">
730:                         <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-6">
731:                             
732:                             <div className={`border border-theme-border p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[var(--radius-box)] flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 justify-between items-center shadow-2xl transition-colors duration-500 bg-theme-card backdrop-blur-[var(--glass-blur)] ${isEditMode ? 'ring-4 ring-amber-500' : ''}`}>
733:                                 <div className="w-full md:flex-1 text-center md:text-left">
734:                                     {isEditMode ? (
735:                                         <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 w-full">
736:                                             <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
737:                                                 <div>
738:                                                     <label className="text-[8px] text-amber-500 uppercase font-black block mb-1">Mali Naslov (Iznad pozdrava)</label>
739:                                                     <input value={uiPostavke.glavni_naslov} onChange={e => setUiPostavke({...uiPostavke, glavni_naslov: e.target.value})} className="bg-black/40 text-blue-400 text-xs uppercase font-black tracking-widest p-2.5 rounded-lg outline-none w-full border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-500" />
740:                                                 </div>
741:                                                 <div>
742:                                                     <label className="text-[8px] text-amber-500 uppercase font-black block mb-1">Pozdravna Poruka</label>
743:                                                     <input value={uiPostavke.pozdravna_poruka} onChange={e => setUiPostavke({...uiPostavke, pozdravna_poruka: e.target.value})} className="bg-black/40 text-slate-300 text-xs p-2.5 rounded-lg outline-none w-full border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-500" />
744:                                                 </div>
745:                                             </div>
746:                                             <div className="border-t border-amber-500/30 pt-3 mt-1">
747:                                                 <label className="text-[9px] text-amber-400 uppercase font-black block mb-2">🔐 Postavke Login Ekrana (Pristup Sistemu)</label>
748:                                                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-6 gap-2 items-end">
749:                                                     <div>
750:                                                         <label className="text-[8px] text-slate-400 uppercase block mb-1">Naziv 1 (Bijelo)</label>
751:                                                         <input value={uiPostavke.login_naslov_1 ?? ''} onChange={e => setUiPostavke({...uiPostavke, login_naslov_1: e.target.value})} placeholder="Smart" className="bg-black/40 text-white text-xs font-black p-2 rounded-lg outline-none w-full border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-500" />
752:                                                     </div>
753:                                                     <div>
754:                                                         <label className="text-[8px] text-slate-400 uppercase block mb-1">Naziv 2 (U boji)</label>
755:                                                         <input value={uiPostavke.login_naslov_2 ?? ''} onChange={e => setUiPostavke({...uiPostavke, login_naslov_2: e.target.value})} placeholder="Timber" className="bg-black/40 text-blue-400 text-xs font-black p-2 rounded-lg outline-none w-full border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-500" />
756:                                                     </div>
757:                                                     <div>
758:                                                         <label className="text-[8px] text-slate-400 uppercase block mb-1">Polje Korisnik</label>
759:                                                         <input value={uiPostavke.login_user_label || ''} onChange={e => setUiPostavke({...uiPostavke, login_user_label: e.target.value})} className="bg-black/40 text-slate-200 text-xs p-2 rounded-lg outline-none w-full border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-500" />
760:                                                     </div>
761:                                                     <div>
762:                                                         <label className="text-[8px] text-slate-400 uppercase block mb-1">Polje Šifra</label>
763:                                                         <input value={uiPostavke.login_pass_label || ''} onChange={e => setUiPostavke({...uiPostavke, login_pass_label: e.target.value})} className="bg-black/40 text-slate-200 text-xs p-2 rounded-lg outline-none w-full border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-500" />
764:                                                     </div>
765:                                                     <div>
766:                                                         <label className="text-[8px] text-slate-400 uppercase block mb-1">Tekst Dugmeta</label>
767:                                                         <input value={uiPostavke.login_btn_tekst || ''} onChange={e => setUiPostavke({...uiPostavke, login_btn_tekst: e.target.value})} className="bg-black/40 text-slate-200 text-xs p-2 rounded-lg outline-none w-full border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-500" />
768:                                                     </div>
769:                                                     <div>
770:                                                         <label className="text-[8px] text-slate-400 uppercase block mb-1">Boja Login-a</label>
771:                                                         <input type="color" value={uiPostavke.login_boja_akcenta || '#2563eb'} onChange={e => setUiPostavke({...uiPostavke, login_boja_akcenta: e.target.value})} className="w-full h-8 rounded cursor-pointer bg-transparent border border-slate-700" />
772:                                                     </div>
773:                                                 </div>
774:                                             </div>
775:                                         </div>
776:                                     ) : (
777:                                         <>
778:                                             <p className="text-theme-muted text-[10px] uppercase font-black tracking-widest mb-1">{uiPostavke.glavni_naslov}</p>
779:                                             <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl text-theme-text font-black">Zdravo, {loggedUser?.ime_prezime || 'Korisnik'}</h1>
780:                                             <p className="text-theme-muted text-sm mt-2">{uiPostavke.pozdravna_poruka}</p>
781:                                         </>
782:                                     )}
783:                                 </div>
784:                                 <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-center w-full md:w-auto justify-end shrink-0">
785:                                     <div className="bg-theme-panel border border-theme-border px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-center md:text-right hidden md:block shadow-inner">
786:                                         <p className="text-[10px] uppercase font-black text-slate-500">Današnji Datum</p>
787:                                         <p className="text-theme-text font-black text-sm">{new Date().toLocaleDateString('bs-BA')}</p>
788:                                     </div>
789:                                     {(loggedUser?.uloga === 'superadmin' || loggedUser?.uloga === 'admin') && (
790:                                         isEditMode ? (
791:                                             <div className="flex gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
792:                                                 <button onClick={odustaniOdEdita} className="flex-1 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-red-900/30 text-red-400 font-black text-[10px] uppercase hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white transition-all shadow-lg border border-red-500/30">✕ ODUSTANI</button>
793:                                                 <button onClick={spasiDizajn} className="flex-[2] px-6 py-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white font-black text-[10px] uppercase hover:bg-emerald-500 transition-all shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(16,185,129,0.5)]">💾 SPASI EKRAN</button>
794:                                             </div>
795:                                         ) : (
796:                                             <button onClick={pokreniEdit} className="w-full sm:w-auto px-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-500 font-black text-[10px] uppercase border border-amber-500/30 hover:bg-amber-500 hover:text-white transition-all shadow-lg">✏️ UREDI EKRAN</button>
797:                                         )
798:                                     )}
799:                                 </div>
800:                             </div>
801: 
802:                             {isEditMode && (
803:                                 <div className="bg-amber-950/20 border-2 border-amber-500/50 p-4 sm:p-6 rounded-[2rem] shadow-2xl space-y-4">
804:                                     <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between sm:items-center border-b border-amber-500/20 pb-4 gap-3">
805:                                         <h3 className="text-amber-500 font-black uppercase text-xs">🛠️ Enterprise Dashboard Builder</h3>
806:                                         <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto min-w-0">
807:                                             <select id="newWidgetSelect" className="flex-1 min-w-0 w-full sm:w-auto bg-theme-panel text-amber-400 p-2.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-black uppercase outline-none border border-amber-500/50 cursor-pointer truncate">
808:                                                 <option value="">-- Odaberi novi Widget --</option>
809:                                                 {DOSTUPNI_WIDGETI.map(w => <option key={w.type} value={w.type}>{w.name}</option>)}
810:                                             </select>
811:                                             <button onClick={() => { const sel = document.getElementById('newWidgetSelect').value; if(sel) addWidget(sel); }} className="shrink-0 bg-amber-600 text-black px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-black uppercase shadow-lg hover:bg-amber-500 transition-colors">➕ DODAJ</button>
812:                                         </div>
813:                                     </div>
814:                                     <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-500/70 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-center">Prevucite blokove (☰) ispod da biste im promijenili redoslijed na ekranu.</p>
815:                                 </div>
816:                             )}
817: 
818:                             <div className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-6 relative z-10 items-stretch">
819:                                 {(uiPostavke.widget_layout || []).map((widget, index) => {
820:                                     if (!isEditMode && loggedUser.uloga !== 'superadmin' && loggedUser.uloga !== 'admin') {
821:                                         if (!widget.allowedRoles?.includes(loggedUser.uloga)) return null;
822:                                     }
823: 
824:                                     const content = renderWidgetContent(widget, index);
825:                                     if (!content && !isEditMode) return null;
826: 
827:                                     return (
828:                                         <div 
829:                                             key={widget.id} 
830:                                             className={`${widget.span} relative flex flex-col h-full min-h-[150px] transition-all duration-300 ${isEditMode ? 'border-2 border-dashed border-amber-500 p-3 bg-black/20 rounded-[2rem]' : 'bg-theme-card rounded-[2rem] border border-theme-border shadow-xl overflow-hidden'} ${isDraggingWidget && widgetDragItem.current === index ? 'opacity-40 scale-95' : ''}`}
831:                                             draggable={isEditMode}
832:                                             onDragStart={(e) => handleWidgetDragStart(e, index)}
833:                                             onDragEnter={(e) => handleWidgetDragEnter(e, index)}
834:                                             onDragEnd={handleWidgetDrop}
835:                                             onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
836:                                         >
837:                                             {isEditMode && (
838:                                                 <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 left-2 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between sm:items-center gap-2 z-[100] bg-black/80 p-2 sm:p-3 rounded-xl backdrop-blur-md border border-amber-500/50 shadow-xl">
839:                                                     <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-500 font-black cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing flex items-center gap-1 uppercase tracking-widest px-2 py-1 hover:bg-amber-900/50 rounded transition-colors self-start sm:self-auto">
840:                                                         <Move size={12}/> Pomicaj
841:                                                     </span>
842:                                                     
843:                                                     <div className="flex flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap gap-2 items-center justify-between w-full sm:w-auto">
844:                                                         <div className="flex items-center gap-1 sm:gap-2 sm:border-r border-slate-700 sm:pr-3 sm:mr-1 flex-1 sm:flex-none justify-center">
845:                                                             <span className="text-[8px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-black hidden sm:block"><Eye size={10} className="inline mr-1"/> Vide:</span>
846:                                                             {['superadmin', 'admin', 'manager', 'operater'].map(uloga => (
847:                                                                 <label key={uloga} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] text-slate-300 font-bold cursor-pointer hover:text-white transition-colors">
848:                                                                     <input 
849:                                                                         type="checkbox" 
850:                                                                         checked={(widget.allowedRoles || []).includes(uloga) || uloga === 'superadmin' || uloga === 'admin'} 
851:                                                                         onChange={() => toggleWidgetRole(index, uloga)} 
852:                                                                         className="w-2.5 h-2.5 sm:w-3 sm:h-3 accent-amber-500" 
853:                                                                         disabled={uloga === 'superadmin' || uloga === 'admin'} 
854:                                                                     />
855:                                                                     {uloga.substring(0,3).toUpperCase()}
856:                                                                 </label>
857:                                                             ))}
858:                                                         </div>
859:                                                         
860:                                                         <div className="flex gap-1.5 items-center shrink-0">
861:                                                             <button onClick={() => updateWidgetSpan(index)} className="bg-theme-panel text-amber-400 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] font-black uppercase border border-amber-500/50 hover:bg-amber-500 hover:text-black transition-colors shadow-sm">
862:                                                                 ŠIRINA: {widget.span.includes('col-span-4') ? '1/3' : (widget.span.includes('col-span-6') ? '1/2' : 'FULL')}
863:                                                             </button>
864:                                                             <button onClick={() => deleteWidget(index)} className="bg-red-900/50 text-red-500 w-6 h-6 sm:w-7 sm:h-7 rounded flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white transition-colors border border-red-500/30">
865:                                                                 <X size={12}/>
866:                                                             </button>
867:                                                         </div>
868:                                                     </div>
869:                                                 </div>
870:                                             )}
871:                                             <div className={`flex-1 h-full w-full relative ${isEditMode ? 'mt-12 pointer-events-none opacity-50' : 'p-6'}`}>
872:                                                 {content}
873:                                             </div>
874:                                         </div>
875:                                     );
876:                                 })}
877:                             </div>
878:                         </div>
879:                     </div>
880:                 ) : activeModule === 'prijem' ? <PrijemModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
881:                 : activeModule === 'prorez' ? <ProrezModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
882:                 : activeModule === 'pilana' ? <PilanaModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
883:                 : activeModule === 'dorada' ? <DoradaModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
884:                 : activeModule === 'planiranje' ? <PlaniranjeModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
885:                 : activeModule === 'lager' ? <LagerPaketaModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
886:                 : activeModule === 'ponude' ? <PonudeModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
887:                 : activeModule === 'radni_nalozi' ? <RadniNaloziModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
888:                 : activeModule === 'otpremnice' ? <OtpremniceModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
889:                 : activeModule === 'racuni' ? <RacuniModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
890:                 : activeModule === 'blagajna' ? <BlagajnaModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
891:                 : activeModule === 'toranj' ? <KontrolniToranjModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
892:                 : (activeModule === 'analitika' || activeModule === 'dashboard') ? <AnalitikaModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
893:                 : activeModule === 'kiosk' ? <KioskModule />
894:                 : activeModule === 'hr' ? <HrDashboardModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
895:                 : activeModule === 'reklamacije' ? <ReklamacijeModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
896:                 : activeModule === 'podesavanja' ? <SettingsModule onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
897:                 : activeModule === 'nadzor' && loggedUser?.uloga === 'superadmin' ? <NadzorModule user={loggedUser} header={header} setHeader={setHeader} onExit={() => setActiveModule('home')} />
898:                 : null}
899:             </div>
900:         </AppShell>
901:     );
902: }
